		<item>
			<title>Johannesburg</title>
			<link>https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Johannesburg</link>
			<guid isPermaLink="false">https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Johannesburg</guid>
			<description>&lt;p&gt;FDSMDP: &lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr /&gt;
&lt;div&gt;{{Infobox location&lt;br /&gt;
|games            =none&lt;br /&gt;
|mentions         =FOTV&lt;br /&gt;
|type             =&lt;br /&gt;
|image            =VSSG Wall Street.png&lt;br /&gt;
|image desc       =Johannesburg time on the [[Wall Street]] stock exchange&lt;br /&gt;
|part of          =South Africa&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Johannesburg&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;, or &amp;quot;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Jo&amp;#039;Burg&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;,&amp;quot; was a city in the pre-[[Great War|War]] [[Pre-War countries|country]] of South Africa.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
In the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Vault Seller&amp;#039;s Survival Guide]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039;, a series of training videos produced by the [[Vault-Tec Corporation]], the [[Wall Street|Wall Street stock exchange]] in [[New York City]] is depicted as having a clock corresponding to the local time in Johannesburg on its wall.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Appearances==&lt;br /&gt;
Johannesburg is mentioned only in the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Vault Seller&amp;#039;s Survival Guide&amp;#039;&amp;#039; episode &amp;quot;[[Steer the Ship!]].&amp;quot;&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
[[Category:Fallout TV series mentioned-only locations]]&lt;br /&gt;
[[Category:Pre-War cities]]&lt;/div&gt;</description>

		<item>
			<title>Fallout 4 plot</title>
			<link>https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Fallout_4_plot</link>
			<guid isPermaLink="false">https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Fallout_4_plot</guid>
			<description>&lt;p&gt;SkyrimsLord13: Page creation&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr /&gt;
&lt;div&gt;This page contains a summary of the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;plot&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; for &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Fallout 4]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039;. Due to the freeform nature of the game&amp;#039;s quests, this summary is written from the perspective of the player character following the main quest&amp;#039;s linear path to its furthest point before being required to choose a [[Fallout 4 factions|faction]] to support, then pursuing a single faction&amp;#039;s quest arc to its end without branching to support another faction or murdering every NPC in the game world independently.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Prologue==&lt;br /&gt;
===[[War Never Changes]]===&lt;br /&gt;
The player character is either the father or mother within a suburban nuclear family living in the [[Sanctuary Hills]] neighborhood near [[Concord]], [[Massachusetts]]. On the morning of [[Timeline (2050-2099)#2077|October 23, 2077]], the family is getting ready to start their day, drinking their coffee and listen to their robot butler [[Codsworth]] tending to the needs of their infant son [[Shaun]]. One of them meets [[Vault-Tec rep|a representative]] of the [[Vault-Tec Corporation]] at their door as he comes to confirm some final details regarding their recent purchase of residencies at the newly constructed [[Vault 111]] close by. Shortly after going to check on their baby, the parents are alerted by Codsworth to a news broadcast reporting on [[Great War|nuclear strikes]] along the [[East Coast]]. The family takes Shaun and flees their home but are forced to leave their robot behind. They manage to get through the security checkpoint and onto the elevator entrance of the Vault moments before a nuclear blast strikes in the distance.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Inside the underground halls of Vault 111, the family and their neighbors reel from the destruction they witnessed outside as the [[Vault 111 overseer]] and his staff work quickly to get everyone situated, including handing out [[Vault jumpsuit (Fallout 4)|jumpsuits]] for them to wear. The new residents are all ushered to high-tech pods, with [[Vault doctor|a doctor]] claiming it&amp;#039;s simply a decontamination procedure. However, shortly after being closed in, the residents are induced into [[Cryonics|cryo suspension]] as part of the Vault&amp;#039;s [[Vault#True purpose|secret experiment.]]&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
An undisclosed amount of time later, the two parents from Sanctuary Hills awaken in their pods. Then, a man and a woman appear outside, gesturing to the pod containing the parent who took Shaun in their arms when they fled to the Vault. With the other helpless to stop them, they attempt to take Shaun from his parent&amp;#039;s arms and when met with resistance, the man raises a handgun and fatally shoots them. As Shaun is taken away, his other parent gets a close look at their spouse&amp;#039;s killer, including his bald head and prominent scar, as he refers to them as &amp;quot;the backup&amp;quot; before leaving. Moments later, the cryo suspension is reactivated, and they fade back into unconsciousness, looking across at their partner&amp;#039;s dead body.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Act 1 - Reunions==&lt;br /&gt;
===[[Out of Time]]===&lt;br /&gt;
Known from now on as the [[Sole Survivor]], they awaken for the second time to discover the Vault in a state of disrepair, with alarms blaring and the computer declaring emergency malfunctions in the cryo array. They escape their own pod and try to get into their dead spouse&amp;#039;s, but find there is nothing to be done; they have already expired. As they navigate through the Vault, the Sole Survivor discovers signs that the staff and other Vault residents have died, leaving them all alone. They are forced to go the long way around the Vault after encountering a malfunctioning bulkhead door, where they encounter [[Radroach (Fallout 4)|radroaches]] and pick up a few loose supplies including weapons and [[Stimpak (Fallout 4)|stimpaks]]. From the late overseer&amp;#039;s office, they get through a security tunnel that leads to the entrance lobby, where they acquire a [[Pip-Boy 3000 Mark IV]] to help them reactive the mechanism to open the blast door. They board the ancient entrance elevator and ascend to the surface.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Outside Vault 111, the Sole Survivor observes the devastated landscape of [[the Commonwealth]]. They descend down the hill back to their neighborhood in Sanctuary Hills, where they discover a functioning Codsworth residing outside their ruined former residence. From the robot butler, the Sole Survivor learns that 210 years have passed since they fled from the falling bombs and it is now [[Timeline (2250-2300)#2287|2287]]. They convince Codsworth to reveal the truth about his unusual cheery behavior and learn that he has been lonely ever since being separated from the family. He offers to help look for the missing Shaun, but The search around the neighborhood ends up fruitless. Though disheartened, Codsworth suggests the Sole Survivor try to speak to survivors allegedly residing in Concord nearby.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Leaving their former neighborhood across the [[Old North Bridge]], the Sole Survivor comes across a [[Red Rocket truck stop]] where they encounter [[Dogmeat (Fallout 4)|a stray dog]] who is happy to see them and they opt to bring him along as a [[Fallout 4 companions|companion]]. Making it to the Concord town limits, the Sole Survivor is drawn to gunfire outside the [[Museum of Freedom]], where they encounter a band of [[Raiders (Fallout 4)|raiders]] attacking a [[Preston Garvey|duster-wearing man]] wielding a [[Laser musket (Fallout 4)|laser mustket]]. After clearing the raiders, the man asks for the Sole Survivor&amp;#039;s help protecting a group of refugees inside the Museum.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===[[Jewel of the Commonwealth]]===&lt;br /&gt;
Whether through their efforts to help the refugees in Concord, speaking to other wasteland inhabitants they come across in their travels, or simply heading in the direction of the [[Boston]] cityscape, the Sole Survivor is drawn to the heart of the Commonwealth&amp;#039;s rough form of civilization known as the &amp;quot;Great Green Jewel,&amp;quot; the settlement of [[Diamond City]] built within the pre-War Fenway Park baseball stadium nestled within the downtown area.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Outside the massive metal gate, they encounter [[Piper Wright]], owner and journalist of the city&amp;#039;s &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Publick Occurrences]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039; newspaper outlet, in a squabble with the settlement leadership who had banned her from the settlement. She ropes the Sole Survivor into a ruse to get them both into Diamond City and although they are stopped at the gate by incumbent DC mayor [[McDonough]], the Sole Survivor is nonetheless allowed entrance into the Green Jewel.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
From talking to any of its residents including Piper, McDonough, and/or the merchants operating in [[Diamond City market|the local market]], the Sole Survivor learns that the one to talk to about locating a missing person around those parts is [[Nick Valentine]], a private investigator. However, upon locating the [[Valentine Detective Agency]], they are told by Nick&amp;#039;s secretary [[Ellie Perkins]] that the detective has been missing for two weeks. They convince her to help them help her boss, so she reveals Nick was working on a case involving the mobster [[Skinny Malone]] and his gang of [[Triggermen]] operating out of [[Park Street station]] near [[Boston Common]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===[[Unlikely Valentine]]===&lt;br /&gt;
The Sole Survivor works their way through the streets of Boston, plagued by many dangers from raiders, to [[Feral ghoul (Fallout 4)|feral ghouls]], to [[Super mutant (Fallout 4)|super mutants]] to reach the surface entrance of Park Street station. Below the streets, they cross through the subway tunnels of the old station until discovering the reason for the Triggermen&amp;#039;s presence, a stronghold based out of the incomplete [[Vault 114]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Inside the old Vault, they contend with more Triggermen littered throughout its halls until finally reaching the overseer&amp;#039;s office where Nick is being held captive by Skinny&amp;#039;s lieutenant [[Dino (Fallout 4)|Dino]]. They unlock the door to his impromptu cell but discover that the private investigator is actually a [[synth]]. Nick waves off further questioning for the time being and urges an immediate exit from the Vault before the rest of Skinny&amp;#039;s gang comes for them.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Though they manage to fight their way through another contingent of Triggermen and return to the Vault entrance, it happens that Skinny Malone had already prepared an ambush. The resulting conversation concludes with either the deaths of Skinny and his gun moll [[Darla (Fallout 4)|Darla]], or Skinny finding reason to give Nick and the Sole Survivor a ten-second window to leave the Vault and get out of his sight. Once out of the subterranean tunnels via an access hatch, Nick questions how the Sole Survivor was able to locate him and learns their reason in needing his help to look for Shaun. The detective agrees to help and invites them to meet up back at his office in Diamond City.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===[[Getting a Clue]]===&lt;br /&gt;
After coming back to the Green Jewel, the Sole Survivor observes Nick&amp;#039;s return. Though Ellie suggests bringing them on as a new partner, Nick puts the discussion aside to instead focus on the new case and invites them to take a seat. Through exhaustive discussion, the Sole Survivor relates all of the detailed information about Shaun&amp;#039;s kidnapping to the detective, though Nick is unable to derive any workable leads on where to start the search. But once they give the physical description of the man who murdered their spouse, he gets curious and has Ellie bring him the casefile on the mercenary [[Conrad Kellogg]]. Along with a match to the physical description, Nick also reveals that Kellogg had a house right there in Diamond City where he lived with who appeared to be a ten-year-old boy. The more questions raised by this compels Nick and his client to find a way into [[Kellogg&amp;#039;s house]] for the answers.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Whether through their own lockpicking skills or acquiring the associated key from one of a few sources, the Sole Survivor and Nick get inside Kellogg&amp;#039;s house. The search initially turns up nothing until they find a switch to a false wall, where Kellogg&amp;#039;s mercenary cache is kept. Among the goods, they find some discarded [[San Francisco Sunlights]] cigars. Nick suggests giving them to Dogmeat, the mutt encountered at the Red Rocket, to use his sharp sense of smell to track down the mercenary. He also offers his own company to help them see this road to the end.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===[[Reunions]]===&lt;br /&gt;
From Diamond City&amp;#039;s gates, Dogmeat leads the Sole Survivor&amp;#039;s journey along old train tracks heading west out of Boston. Along the way, they encounter a few places where Kellogg&amp;#039;s route took him, from brief rest stops to sites of battle against hazards like mutated animals and raiders, and where he left more clues useful for giving to Dogmeat to pick up his scent. At the end of the hunt, Dogmeat brings them to the exterior of the old military base at [[Fort Hagen]] where automated turrets guard the perimeter keeping intruders out. But the Sole Survivor still finds a way inside. They get full confirmation of Kellogg&amp;#039;s presence from the guard contingents of mechanical Gen 2 synths sent to deter them moving inside the fort.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The Sole Survivor gets past the synths guarding the upper floors to find their way into the [[Fort Hagen Command Center|underground command center]]. Their progress is watched by Kellogg himself, who utilizes the loudspeaker system to taunt them and warn against continuing to look for him. But the Sole Survivor is undeterred and eventually reaches the main terminal hub, which Kellogg commends them for their tenacity by having his remaining synth guards stand down so they can talk. The mercenary reveals that Shaun is no longer in his possession and that he turned him over to his employer, [[the Institute]], a secretive organization feared across the Commonwealth. He gives the Sole Survivor one final warning that they are not ready for what comes of trying to find the Institute, then duels them in a fight ending with his death. The Sole Survivor extracts a few pieces of cybernetic hardware from his corpse including a [[Cybernetic brain augmenter|brain augmenter]], then looks into Kellogg&amp;#039;s personal files to confirm that he had been working for the Institute and they now had Shaun. Shortly after leaving the fort via a roof access elevator, they bear witness to the arrival of [[The Prydwen|the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039;]], a massive airship piloted by the [[Brotherhood of Steel (Fallout 4)|Brotherhood of Steel]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Left with no leads once again, the Sole Survivor returns to Diamond City to meet with Piper and Nick at her newspaper office, thinking her investigation into the organization could help. However, while Piper has nothing to add, Nick&amp;#039;s examination of Kellogg&amp;#039;s cybernetic remains inspires him to suggest he and the Sole Survivor take it to an expert, [[Amari|Doctor Amari]], who resides within the [[Drifter (Fallout 4)|drifter]] community of [[Goodneighbor]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Act 2 - Finding the Institute==&lt;br /&gt;
===[[Dangerous Minds]]===&lt;br /&gt;
After going further into the urban area, the Sole Survivor reaches the gate of Goodneighbor built around the [[Old State House]]. They were &amp;quot;welcomed&amp;quot; by the conman [[Finn (Fallout 4)|Finn]] who tries to extort them, but the situation can end up dealt with by Goodneighbor&amp;#039;s [[ghoul]] mayor, [[John Hancock]] who is more receptive. They navigate through to the [[Memory Den]], an establishment based on reliving one&amp;#039;s past memories through technology. There, with Nick in mandatory attendance, the two are shown to Amari in the basement by the Den&amp;#039;s owner [[Irma]]. There, Amari examines the cybernetic piece and deduces that its contents are still intact, but accessing them is nearly impossible with her technology alone. She instead suggests using Nick himself as a sort-of middleman while the Sole Survivor dives into Kellogg&amp;#039;s memories via the [[memory lounger]]s. With Nick&amp;#039;s consent, they enter a lounger and Amari inducts them into the late mercenary&amp;#039;s mind.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Through the process of viewing several important memories, the Sole Survivor learns about Kellogg&amp;#039;s past including: growing up in the [[New California Republic|NCR]] with his parents; moving to [[San Francisco]] as a young adult with [[Sarah (Fallout 4)|a wife]] and [[Mary (Fallout 4)|a daughter]] of his own; going on a bloody rampage after unknown enemies killed them in retaliation against him; his time wandering east as an apathetic mercenary taking any job no matter the body count; his first meeting with the Institute where they wanted him dead until he convinced them he was more valuable alive and working for them; and then, that fateful day in Vault 111 where he destroyed a family himself by killing one parent, taking their child, and sparing the life of the other.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Then, they find the most recent memory, during the time Kellogg was sheltering in Diamond City with who appears to be a ten-year-old Shaun, grown up into adolescence. They were visited by a [[courser]], an elite agent of the Institute, who delivered to Kellogg the details on a new job for him involving tracking down a scientist from the Institute who went rogue and fled. The agent then took the young Shaun back to the Institute via a means of [[Teleportation#The Institute|teleportation]] technology, unknowingly exposing the real reason why nobody has been able to find the organization in all this time.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
After exiting the lounger, the Sole Survivor discusses with Amari and determines that their next step is finding the rogue Institute scientist, [[Brian Virgil]] and getting him to talk. However, Amari warns them of the potential danger, as the memory revealed Virigil had somehow fled into the depths of the [[Glowing Sea]], a region to the southwest infamously known as a heavily [[Radiation|irradiated]] hellscape home to many mutated horrors.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===[[The Glowing Sea]]===&lt;br /&gt;
Once preparations are made, the Sole Survivor makes the journey to the southwest corner of the Commonwealth where they arrive at the [[edge of the Glowing Sea]]. Braving the constant radiation levels and the monsters that roam through the devastated area, they make their way to the heart of the region at a massive impact crater, only to find it inhabited by members of the [[Church of the Children of Atom]] who worship the radiation as a blessing from their god &amp;quot;Atom.&amp;quot; Their leader [[Isolde]] is persuaded to provide information on Virgil and points the Sole Survivor towards a [[rocky cave]] further southwest.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Inside the cave sanctuary, the Sole Survivor discovers Virgil and finds out he accomplished surviving in the Glowing Sea due to turning himself into a super mutant. Virgil initially suspects them as an agent of the Institute helping Kellogg to hunt him down but is convinced otherwise in short order. Once he finds out they are trying to find a way inside the Institute, he is initially skeptial of their chances. But this vanishes once he learns the reason and agrees to help on the condition they help him in return by acquiring an [[experimental serum]] from the Institute that he says will help cure him of his [[Forced Evolutionary Virus|FEV]] mutation.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
He goes on to explain the mechanism of the Institute&amp;#039;s teleportation technology, the &amp;quot;Molecular Relay,&amp;quot; and how the Sole Survivor is going to hijack it by building a [[signal interceptor]] that will instead teleport them into the underground facility. Before that, however, they need to obtain a vital piece of tech: a [[Courser chip|specialized chip]] implanted into a courser that can access the Relay, which will require finding one and killing it to get its chip. He points them toward the [[C.I.T. ruins]], situated directly above the Institute itself, with knowledge of using a trick with their Pip-Boy&amp;#039;s radio in order to find a courser.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===[[Hunter Hunted (Fallout 4)|Hunter/Hunted]]===&lt;br /&gt;
At the C.T.T. ruins in [[Cambridge]], the Sole Survivor utilizes their Pip-Boy radio and the trick to follow an invisible trail taking them east until arriving on the steps of the [[Greenetech Genetics]]. Inside it, they discover the remains of a salvage team led by the [[Gunners]] mercenaries, with their leader using the building loudspeakers to call out the position of the courser who is on a path of carnage through their defensive line, seemingly seeking to acquire a girl the mercenaries have captured. The Sole Survivor themselves is also targeted as a suspected hostile while they try to chase after the Institute agent.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
On the top floor of the structure, the Sole Survivor comes upon the courser, [[Z2-47]] as he is torturing the remaining Gunners to get the password for the cell containing the runaway synth he was tracking. When confronted, the courser reveals he had already found out they were stalking him and tries to find out why, but regardless of the answer intends to kill them to clean up loose ends, a fight that does not go in his favor.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
With the courser chip in their possession, the Sole Survivor brings it back to Amari in Goodneighbor, however, she admits that its level of encryption is beyond her capabilities. Fortunately, she also knows someone who can help: [[the Railroad]], another secret organization who have dedicated themselves to opposing the Institute and freeing the synths they believe suffer in slavery to their creators. Though she does not know where they are based, she gives the Sole Survivor a clue to finding them, saying to &amp;quot;follow the [[Freedom Trail]].&amp;quot;&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===[[The Molecular Level]]===&lt;br /&gt;
Taking Amari&amp;#039;s advice, the Sole Survivor locates the start of the historical Freedom Trail monument and discovers painted markings on each of the metal plates that translate to a coded password, with the Trail ultimately taking them to the door of the [[Old North Church]]. In the lower catacombs filled with feral ghouls, they find a rigged-up decoder ring and input the password &amp;quot;Railroad.&amp;quot; Inside the connected chamber, they are found by armed members of the Railroad including its leader, [[Desdemona]]. She demands to know who they are that would go to all the trouble of trying to find their group but is surprised to learn that they came to the Railroad to decode a courser chip. With additional convincing from her right-hand, [[Deacon]], Desdemona allows the Sole Survivor to enter their [[Railroad HQ|headquarters]]. There, the chip is given to their resident tech genius, [[Tinker Tom]] who is able to crack its encryption. While Desdemona hands over the decrypted data, she demands the chip itself be left with them, and if done so, extends an offer for the Sole Survivor to work more closely with the Railroad.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Upon returning back to Virgil in the Glowing Sea, he is surprised to hear they were able to defeat a courser but admits it is impressive, nonetheless. He then reveals that he spent that time compiling a set of rough blueprints for the signal interceptor from what little he could remember about it during his time in the Institute. He advises working with one of the major factions who can provide the resources and location needed to build the signal interceptor in safety, then reminds the Sole Survivor about their deal to get him the serum he needs.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Once the Sole Survivor progresses their involvement with one of the major factions past a certain point, they approach the relevant authority figure ([[Arthur Maxson]], Desdemona, or Preston Garvey) to tell them about the goal to infiltrate the Institute. Through working with a faction&amp;#039;s technical genius, they are able to assemble the necessary parts of the signal interceptor, for which they can only use once with the courser chip data. Before the plan goes through, the Sole Survivor is given a mission by their chosen faction to use a [[network scanner]] to scrape vital data from the Institute&amp;#039;s computers and give them more information about the enemy. With the go-ahead, the Sole Survivor is teleported through the Molecular Relay into the Institute.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===[[Institutionalized]]===&lt;br /&gt;
After being deposited in the chamber of the Molecular Relay within the underground facility, the Sole Survivor is greeted over loudspeaker by Father, the leader of the Institute. He bids them to remain calm and follow his directions so they can talk. As the Sole Survivor follows the path Father guides them along, he talks about the true mission of the Institute, how they are working to benefit the cause of humanity in the wasteland despite what the wasteland itself thinks of them, but stops from going further as he knows their real mission in getting inside was to find their son. At the end of the path, the Sole Survivor discovers the ten-year-old Shaun inside a sealed room, however, when they try to get him to come out, Shaun only becomes confused and calling out for Father to help him. Father himself enters the room a short time later and uses a recall code to deactivate Shaun, revealed to actually be a synth.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Knowing the Sole Survivor&amp;#039;s emotions are on edge, Father tries to keep things peaceful as he reveals the truth: their original belief that only ten years had passed between Shaun being taken from them and their escape from Vault 111 was incorrect; in actuality it had been sixty years, Shaun was now an old man, and known by a different name, &amp;quot;Father,&amp;quot; the current director of the Institute. Should the Sole Survivor remain peaceful as he wanted, they discuss how their son came to be in charge of the organization that kidnapped him from his parents. Shaun does not deny the moral question, but downplays it in favor of espousing that it was ultimately the best choice because it led to him becoming director and leading the Institute in trying to change the surface for the better. He invites the Sole Survivor to join him and rekindle some kind of family relationship they never got to have before while furthering the Institute&amp;#039;s mission.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
As a show of good faith, Shaun instructs them to tour the Institute and meet with its various division heads to get a better sense of the organization&amp;#039;s inner workings, hoping they will be convinced that helping the Institute will also be in their best interests. One of the Sole Survivor&amp;#039;s stops along their tour is with the head of [[Institute Advanced Systems|Advanced Systems]], [[Madison Li|Dr. Madison Li]], who upgrades their Pip-Boy to allow them to utilize the Molecular Relay at will, a privilege very few have and taken as a sign of Father&amp;#039;s trust in them.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Act 3 - Picking a Side==&lt;br /&gt;
===The Minutemen===&lt;br /&gt;
====[[When Freedom Calls]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Upon discovering the situation in Concord, the Sole Survivor heads into the Museum of Freedom and thins out the raiders&amp;#039; numbers including two attempting to break into the upstairs office where the refugee group is sheltering. There, they properly meet Preston Garvey who identifies himself as a member of a group called the [[Commonwealth Minutemen]], who once protected the Commonwealth at large until they fell apart from infighting and are largely inactive by the present. He then relates the plan he is currently working on to get out of danger involving getting a [[Fusion core (Fallout 4)|fusion core]] from the Museum&amp;#039;s working generator to power up a suit of [[T-45 power armor (Fallout 4)|T-45 power armor]] residing on the rooftop along with acquiring the [[Minigun (Fallout 4)|minigun]] on a wrecked [[Vertibird (Fallout 4)|vertibird]]. The Sole Survivor agrees to help them out of their predicament.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Once the fusion core is obtained, the Sole Survivor heads up to the roof via third-floor access, dons both the T-45 armor and the minigun and jumps into combat with the raiders. However, the situation becomes more dangerous partway through the fight; one of the refugees, [[Mama Murphy]] can allege to the Sole Survivor that a precognitive ability she calls &amp;quot;The Sight&amp;quot; revealed to her something dangerous was coming towards the town. That danger turns out to be a [[Deathclaw (Fallout 4)|deathclaw]] that bursts out of a sewer tunnel connected to the [[Concord Civic Access]] and enters the battle. Despite its strength and some of the T-45 armor pieces being weakened from wear-and-tear, the Sole Survivor manages to defeat the beast and finish off the remaining raiders including their leader [[Gristle]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Regrouping with Preston&amp;#039;s refugees, the Minuteman thanks the Sole Survivor for their assistance and offers [[Bottlecap (Fallout 4)|caps]] as compensation. If they helped out the refugees when first coming through Concord, Mama Murphy offers an additional gift in telling them to look for clues related to Shaun at Diamond City. Though Preston admits it might be asking too much, he requests the Sole Survivor to come with them as he is taking the group to a location Mama Murphy saw through the Sight that would be safe for them to shelter in, &amp;quot;Sanctuary.&amp;quot; The Sole Survivor agrees to go with them to Preston&amp;#039;s delight.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
After leaving the streets of Concord, the group walks northwest where it turns out &amp;quot;Sanctuary&amp;quot; is their former neighborhood of Sanctuary Hills. While the other refugees settle in, the Sole Survivor speaks with Preston again where he thanks them for getting his people to safety at last. He additionally asks for them to work with mechanic [[Sturges]] on making a functional [[Fallout 4 settlements|settlement]] for the new residents out of the pre-War houses. Second, he implores the Sole Survivor to look into a request passed through the caravans by another settlement out in the Commonwealth that needed the Minutemen&amp;#039;s help with an important matter.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[The First Step]]====&lt;br /&gt;
The Sole Survivor makes the trek to the settlement requesting help where the residing settlers admit they were initially skeptical that any response from the Minutemen was coming but are glad to see the group stepping up. They reveal they asked for help because a gang of raiders operating near their home has been harassing them and making life difficult. The Sole Survivor agrees to track the gang down and wipe them out in exchange for getting the settlers&amp;#039; support for the Minutemen&amp;#039;s return to form.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
They travel to the location of the raider stronghold and clear it out of its savage occupants, including eliminating the boss in charge, then take the news back to the settlers endangered by their raids and get their help along with unrestricted access to any water and food supplies they keep stored in their [[Workshop (Fallout 4)|workshop]]. The Sole Survivor returns to Preston in Sanctuary to bring him word of the settlement&amp;#039;s support for the Minutemen. Preston thanks them for helping out and reveals he has considered their willingness to help others who ask for it and decides to offer them the vacant position as the Minutemen&amp;#039;s general, leading the group in its revival; he reasons that since there are very few, if any active Minutemen left besides himself, nobody can argue against it. The Sole Survivor agrees to take on the mantle and the responsibilities that come with it.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Taking Independence]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Later on after the Sole Survivor has put their new position as General to good work recruiting at least two more settlements to the Minutemen&amp;#039;s cause, the next time they speak with Preston, he advises that the recent additions to manpower and resources should be used to take firm action in demonstrating that the Minutemen are returning to their former role as protectors of the Commonwealth. He suggests leading a strike force to regain control of [[the Castle]], the Minutemen&amp;#039;s former headquarters long ago, especially for the reason that it houses a powerful radio transmitter that will allow them to react to distress calls region-wide.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
On their General&amp;#039;s orders, Preston and a small team of [[Minuteman|minutemen]] assemble at a staging area just outside the site of the Castle in [[East Boston]], known pre-War as Fort Independence. The Sole Survivor learns that the ancient fortress has been taken over by a [[Mirelurk (Fallout 4)|mirelurk]] nest with multiple clutches of eggs scattered around both the inner courtyard and the interior halls. They hear from their strike team on various strategies for taking the fight to the mirelurks and come to a decision. Following the General&amp;#039;s choice, the Minutemen carry out the assault, eradicating the adult mirelurks while destroying every egg clutch they come across. However, the disturbance caused by their attack awakens the nest&amp;#039;s resident [[Mirelurk (Fallout 4)#Mirelurk queen|queen]] from the ocean waters it was slumbering in, forcing the Minutemen to fight and kill it as well.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Once the last of the mutant crustaceans are cleared out, Preston and the Sole Survivor examine the intact radio transmitter and find it in working order, only lacking a power source. Using the present workshop, they construct generators to power on the transmitter, which begins broadcasting [[Radio Freedom]] throughout the entire Commonwealth. Preston wholeheartedly thanks the General for their efforts putting the Minutemen back on the path they had strayed from and encourages them to continue exploring around and finding more settlements to add to their growing network.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Old Guns]]====&lt;br /&gt;
A short time after news of the Minutemen&amp;#039;s return and reclaiming the Castle spread around the region, the Sole Survivor is alerted to a new arrival at the fort asking to meet them and goes to find the one responsible. There, they find [[Ronnie Shaw]], a veteran member of the Minutemen from its glory days who came to check out the new General for herself. Despite her tough attitude, Ronnie admits they have done good work getting the Castle back in the Minutemen&amp;#039;s control. She reveals her intimate knowledge of the fortress including the cache of weapons and armaments kept stored in the sealed west-wing armory, in particular [[Artillery schematic|schematics]] for building [[Artillery piece (Fallout 4)|artillery embankments]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
With Ronnie&amp;#039;s help, the Sole Survivor excavates a collapsed entryway connected to the Castle&amp;#039;s [[The Castle tunnels|subterranean tunnels]] they need to traverse to get into the armory due to the courtyard entrance being sealed from the inside. Down in the tunnels, they discover someone littered the area with [[land mine]]s and also find [[Sarge (Fallout 4)|Sarge]], an old [[Sentry bot (Fallout 4)|sentry bot]] that formerly defended the Castle. However, the passage of time rendered its identification protocols corrupted, and it attacks them, forcing its destruction. Once Ronnie gets them into the locked section of the tunnels, they discover the preserved body of [[McGann]], the former General who led during her time in the Minutemen and the one who sealed off the armory and laid the land mines in the tunnels to protect its contents when the Castle [[Battle of the Castle|originally fell]] to the mirelurks.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
From the opposite entrance in the tunnels, the Sole Survivor and Ronnie get into the armory and examine the cache contents. Ronnie decides there is enough to work with for getting the Castle properly outfitted with artillery once again and has the General collect the schematic and loose materials to build up at least two artillery pieces on the Castle grounds. Next, she arranges for a test fire of the new artillery by having them throw out [[Artillery smoke grenade (Fallout 4)|special smoke grenades]] to mark a target and listen for a callout on Radio Freedom. Once the display is over, the General speaks with Preston who congratulates them on strengthening the Minutemen even more and advises they build up additional artillery embankments in all their allied settlements to better help protect the entire Commonwealth.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Defend the Castle]]====&lt;br /&gt;
In the event of the Sole Survivor being [[banished from the Institute]] for various reasons, they go to Preston with a request to help stop the organization&amp;#039;s continued terrorizing of the Commonwealth. Preston only agrees to the plan of attack once the Minutemen have [[Form Ranks|sufficient manpower]] to go against their major enemy. However, their planning does not go unheard and soon the Castle is alerted to scouting attempts by the Institute, indicating a major offensive is coming. A broadcast from Radio Freedom asks all members of the Minutemen in the area to assist the Sole Survivor at the Castle. They protect their fortress against waves of Gen 1 and 2 synths as well as coursers until eventually emerging triumphant. In order to keep ahead of a second attack on the Castle, Preston sends the General to confer with Sturges on finding a way to break into the Institute itself.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[The Nuclear Option (Minutemen)|The Nuclear Option]]====&lt;br /&gt;
The Sole Survivor goes to Sturges and discusses his findings regarding the data collected on the network scanner they gave him. The mechanic notifies the General of a possible backdoor into the Institute: an old cooling water tunnel system with a surface access door along the [[Charles River]]. He then gives a holotape with which they can reprogram the Molecular Relay to teleport in Minutemen reinforcements once they are inside the facility, as well as giving insistent warnings of intense radiation in the area they need to traverse.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
From their search in the vicinity of the C.I.T. ruins, the Sole Survivor locates the access door and traverses through the [[public works maintenence area]], dealing with hazards opposing their path including mounted automated turrets, feral ghouls, and [[Mole rat (Fallout 4)|mole rats]] on top of the consistent levels of radiation until they manage to reach the old piping connected directly to the Relay chamber. There, they input the targeting sequence holotape and hijack the Relay to bring in Garvey, Sturges, and a contingent of soldiers. Preston reveals the plan to put a definitive end to the Institute: planting a [[Fusion pulse charge (Fallout 4)|fusion pulse charge]] inside the Institute&amp;#039;s main fusion reactor to destabilize it and wipe out the entire facility in a massive explosion.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Though the attack force has to reroute through a series of old laboratories and the Institute defenders placed there to deter them, they are able to invade the main concourse and face off against a defensive line of Gen 2s and coursers. Sturges directs them to get into Advanced Systems but then discovers it had been locked down from the director&amp;#039;s office. The Sole Survivor goes there themselves where they encounter the bed-ridden Shaun, dying of a terminal illness. Whatever his parent&amp;#039;s response to him, Shaun expresses his disappointment that they have chosen to destroy the Institute and everything he has worked to accomplish. With his credentials, they are able to lift the lockdown and have a secondary option to initiate a facility-wide evacuation order, potentially giving the scientists and civilian members of the Institute a chance to escape. With their route unlocked, the Minutemen push through Advanced Systems and into the reactor chamber. There, they fight the highest concentration of synth defenders including some with advanced weaponry and armor. But it proves not enough as the Sole Survivor plants the fusion pulse charge in the reactor chamber.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Sturges briefly brings the strike force back to the Relay room, where the Sole Survivor discovers the child Shaun synth waiting for them and now fully believing it is their son. Once a decision is made about the synth, Sturges is given the go-ahead to teleport everyone out for the last time. They are then deposited on the top floor of the [[Mass Fusion building]], looking out in the direction of Cambridge and the C.I.T. ruins. With the push of a pre-set detonator, they witness the nuclear explosion engulf the area and cementing the Institute&amp;#039;s destruction once and for all. The Sole Survivor has a momentary reflection on their journey before conferring with Preston, who once more congratulates them on saving the Commonwealth from its greatest enemy. He however admits that even without the Institute&amp;#039;s threat looming over it, there will be plenty more to do to keep the people safe, ensuring the Minutemen will not out of a job any time soon.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[With Our Powers Combined]]====&lt;br /&gt;
In the event of the Sole Survivor doing any action to turn the Brotherhood of Steel hostile against them, when next speaking to Preston, they propose a plan to deal with the militant organization that hoards the Commonwealth&amp;#039;s technology for themselves. Preston suggests a combined artillery salvo to bring down the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039; from a distance. Once enough artillery batteries are constructed across the Minutemen&amp;#039;s network of settlements, the General instructs their operator to send the order over Radio Freedom. In short fashion, the flying fortress is blown out of the sky, with the flaming remains crashing onto the [[Boston Airport]] tarmac below it.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
At the Castle, the Minutemen only get a few moments to celebrate before they spot a fleet of Brotherhood vertibirds approaching from the airport ruins. Another battle to defend the Castle begins as the militia soldiers contend with airborne attacks as well as [[T-60 power armor (Fallout 4)|T60]]-armored knights dropping in to attack on the ground. Despite the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s last-ditch effort, the assault fails and the Minutemen claim total victory. In discussion with Preston, he states that while the Brotherhood has lost their senior officers and command center, there will still be armed squads who avoided the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039;{{&amp;#039;}}s destruction that may continue to pose a threat. Additionally, after a few days, the General is told that the Minutemen have managed to salvage a few functioning vertibirds to add to their arsenal as aerial transport.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===The Brotherhood===&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Fire Support]]====&lt;br /&gt;
At any point before defeating [[Conrad Kellogg]] at [[Fort Hagen]], if the Sole Survivor goes near the western end of [[Cambridge]], their Pip-Boy picks up [[military frequency AF95]], carrying a distress call from one [[Haylen|Scribe Haylen]] sheltering at the [[Cambridge Police Station]]. Heading there leads to discovering an ongoing combat situation between waves of feral ghouls pouring in from the surrounding ruins and a man in T-60 power armor fending them off while protecting an unarmored woman and man behind him. The Sole Survivor jumps in to help eliminate the rest of the creatures and take the pressure off the survivors.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Though the man is thankful, he then turns it around to ask who they are and why they came there. Once given satisfactory answers, he introduces himself as [[Danse|Paladin Danse]] of the [[Brotherhood of Steel (Fallout 4)|Brotherhood of Steel]] and says he and his team, now consisting of himself, Haylen, and [[Rhys|Knight Rhys]], are on a mission of reconnaissance that has been stalled by the present. The Sole Survivor offers to help them out with their mission and Danse invites them to take some of their supplies in the station.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Call to Arms]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Once inside the Police Station and fully prepared, the Sole Survivor talks with Paladin Danse, who reveals their objective is reaching the nearby [[ArcJet Systems]] facility with the aim of obtaining a [[deep range transmitter]] to help contact [[Scabbard]] and report to the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s leadership. With Haylen and Rhys staying behind to defend the station, the paladin takes the lead in traveling out of Cambridge towards the facility. Along the way, he relates to the Sole Survivor some of the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s history in the Commonwealth including that his team is the latest of three to arrive there after the second one went missing, and that their mission&amp;#039;s success became more important after they picked up unusual energy readings in the area with no definitive source. They are briefly interrupted by a gang of raiders trying to loot a couple of scavengers they recently killed that attack them, before proceeding. Upon reaching the steps of ArcJet Systems, Danse stops them to reinforce that once they are inside, his orders are to be followed closely to minimize danger and ensure success.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Inside the pre-War facility, they progress up to the first-floor security room, where Danse stops upon discovering evidence of a battle against the automated security system, which has him claiming it was the work of [[synth]]s sent by [[the Institute]]; he briefly explains their role as a rogue cabal of scientists that have threatened the safety of the Commonwealth with their dangerous technology including the artificial race&amp;#039;s creation. Further in, they momentarily encountered a sealed security door, but upon unlocking it, are set upon by synths. This continues as they push deeper into the facility, along with dealing with the remaining security systems and automated turrets.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Their progress eventually brings them to a massive silo used to test the [[XMB booster engine]] that still hangs from the ceiling, with Danse suggesting finding a way to the top-floor control room that first requires them to restore the power. The Sole Survivor goes ahead by themselves to throw the switch for the building&amp;#039;s auxiliary power system, however, they return to find Danse in the engine room fighting solo against waves of synths teleporting in. The Sole Survivor either joins the fight directly or takes an alternate option of reactivating the XMB booster engine itself to obliterate all the synths at once, which Danse manages to survive thanks to his armor. With the power restored, they use the elevators to reach the top floor and take down the last of the synths who had also come for the transmitter.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
After exiting ArcJet through an emergency access elevator, Danse stops them again to offer his thanks for their assistance despite the Institute&amp;#039;s interference. He then extends two offers to them: first, he offers them a caps reward and his [[Righteous Authority|personal laser rifle]] in exchange for turning over the deep range transmitter. Second, he extends them an offer to give up the mercenary wanderer lifestyle and become a part of something greater in the Brotherhood of Steel.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Semper Invicta]]====&lt;br /&gt;
The Sole Survivor returns to the Cambridge Police Station, where Danse relays the choice they made about his offer of membership to Haylen and Rhys. Once accepted, the paladin assigns them the rank of initiate and he is unable to promote them any higher on his own. He orders the Sole Survivor to become acquainted with how the Brotherhood operates by taking on assignments from Haylen and Rhys, respectively being sent to retrieve uncovered pieces of technology and eradicating nearby threats around the Commonwealth.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Shadow of Steel]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Following the arrival of the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s airship, the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039; to the Commonwealth, the Sole Survivor finds a new message from Danse on the Police Station&amp;#039;s radio frquency requesting them to head there immediately. They discover upon arrival that it has received proper reinforcements from the Brotherhood, with Danse himself then revealing the airship&amp;#039;s arrival means that the supreme commander of the Brotherhood, [[Arthur Maxson|Elder Arthur Maxson]] has come to wage war against the Institute. He orders them to follow him as they take a [[Vertibird (Fallout 4)|vertibird]] and fly across the Commonwealth to dock on board the airship, now moored above the derelict [[Boston Airport]] that the Brotherhood has converted into their regional headquarters.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
On the flight deck of the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039;, the Sole Survivor is introduced to [[Kells|Lancer-Captain Kells]], the airship&amp;#039;s commanding officer, who gives them their new orders to report to Elder Maxson on the command deck as well as a fair warning that whatever their past experiences, they were now a part of a military organization and expected to follow their superiors&amp;#039; orders without question.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
On the command deck, the Sole Survivor witnesses a speech given by the Elder to the ship&amp;#039;s crew, emphasizing the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s mission to safeguard humanity&amp;#039;s future in the Commonwealth by eradicating the Institute and their synth army. In their private conversation, Elder Maxson evaluates the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s newest recruit and indicates he expects to hear great things about them, starting with promoting them to the rank of knight himself and given access to a personal suit of [[Power armor (Fallout 4)|power armor]]. He orders the Sole Survivor to familiarize themselves with the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s senior officers and crew, then report back to him on the flight deck for their next mission.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Tour of Duty]]====&lt;br /&gt;
On the main deck of the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039;, the Sole Survivor introduces themselves to the four senior officers of the Brotherhood: [[Ingram|Proctor Ingram]] who is in charge of keeping the airship running and in top shape; [[Quinlan|Proctor Quinlan]] who is in charge of the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s research and technology recovery efforts; [[Teagan|Proctor Teagan]] who is in charge of logistics and supply management as the quartermaster. Apart from the proctors, the Sole Survivor is also sent to undergo a medical evaluation by [[Cade|Knight-Captain Cade]] to ensure they are completely fit for duty.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Show No Mercy]]====&lt;br /&gt;
With the all-clear from Cade, the Sole Survivor reports to the flight deck where Elder Maxson is standing on an overlook. He acknowledges their interest in taking the fight to the Institute but requires they complete an important mission first and points out the nearby location of [[Fort Strong]], a [[Super mutant (Fallout 4)|super mutant]] stronghold containing intact supplies of [[Fat Man (Fallout 4)|Fat Man]] launchers and mini nukes that would prove a grave security threat if not dealt with.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The Sole Survivor hops on a gunship vertibird and deploys to the skies above the fort, where the mutants try to shoot them down and a [[Super mutant (Fallout 4)#Behemoths|behemoth]] hurls debris like missiles. Eventually, their pilot reports sustained damage on the aircraft, forcing an emergency drop-off in order to return to the airport for repairs. The Sole Survivor finishes eliminating the mutants and taking down the behemoth, then enters the fort&amp;#039;s intact armory building.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
While fighting through more of the mutants inside, the Sole Survivor uses an elevator to descend to its sublevel where the mutant leader resides and finishes their eradication, along with finding the Fat Man stockpile which Danse says he was ordered to secure on the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s behalf and sends them back to the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039;. Back on board, Elder Maxson congratulates their successful mission and grants a reward of [[vertibird signal grenade]]s giving them access to aerial transport. With the immediate security threat over with, the Elder instructs his new knight to continue looking for potential leads on finding the Institute&amp;#039;s main stronghold.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[From Within]]====&lt;br /&gt;
During the construction of the signal interceptor on-site at the airport, the Sole Survivor is given an additional task by Elder Maxson on top of Ingram&amp;#039;s request involving the network scanner. He orders them to locate [[Madison Li]] inside the Institute, a scientist who formerly worked with the Brotherhood in the past and who Maxson now wants to recruit for a &amp;quot;special project.&amp;quot;&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Once inside the Institute and sent by Father to meet with the division heads, Dr. Li is met in the [[Institute Advanced Systems|Advanced Systems]] as its incumbent head. When she is made aware of their allegiances, she initially accuses the Sole Survivor of being sent as an assassin to take her out of the Institute&amp;#039;s hands but is told that is not the case, and instead the Brotherhood requires her expertise for their interests yet again. Though Li admits to having doubts about what the Institute is doing with her work, she demands a favor in exchange for her help first, asking them to locate evidence of the fate of her colleague, Brian Virgil who vanished after an incident in the [[FEV lab|FEV research wing]]. Whether through getting her the evidence of Virgil&amp;#039;s escape or simply persuading her, the doctor is convinced to desert the Institute and return to the Brotherhood.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Alternatively, if the Sole Survivor does not convince Li to desert before being [[banished from the Institute]], she remains inside the facility. Elder Maxson will become furious at their failure and says the Brotherhood will have to rely on finding a suitable replacement roboticist.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Liberty Reprimed]]====&lt;br /&gt;
After handing over the network scanner and its data to Proctor Quinlan for study, the Brotherhood knight is tasked by Elder Maxson to cooperate with Proctor Ingram on the &amp;quot;special project&amp;quot; she has been working on for years by then. At the airport concourse, Ingram welcomes them into the fold and takes them to a secured storage room repurposed to hold the disassembled parts of [[Liberty Prime]], a pre-War robot superweapon the Brotherhood deployed during conflict in the [[Capital Wasteland]] years ago until it was destroyed. Though Ingram has managed to get most of the robot&amp;#039;s hardware rebuilt and improved, critical issues with Prime&amp;#039;s power systems have stalled their efforts, for which they require the assistance of Dr. Madison Li who originally worked on the robot for the Brotherhood.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
If the Sole Survivor was able to convince Li to desert the Institute previously, Ingram reveals that the doctor has refused to collaborate on Prime&amp;#039;s restoration so far and asks the knight to meet with her on board the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039; to change her mind. Li is initially apprehensive about again using her knowledge to create a weapon of war, but the Sole Survivor is able to persuade her to help put an end to the bigger threat of the Institute.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Alternatively, if the mission to recruit Li failed by being banished from the Institute, Ingram points them to the replacement the Brotherhood has found, [[Professor Scara]], an independent researcher operating in [[Diamond City]]. However, they arrive at the [[Science! Center]] to find her missing and her partner [[Duff (Fallout 4)|Doctor Duff]] saying she has left on a research expedition. The knight follows her trail to the [[General Atomics Galleria]], where Scara is sequestered inside the [[Back Alley Bowling]] establishment kept in lockdown by the Galleria&amp;#039;s [[Director (Fallout 4)|Director]] robot, forcing them to find a means to disable the lockdown. Upon speaking with her, the Sole Survivor either persuades her directly or offers her a [[Cerebrofusion adapter|piece of technology]] given by Ingram as a good faith token to stoke her interest and gain her cooperation.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Once the Brotherhood has its roboticist, the reconstruction of Liberty Prime begins. To physically connect his arms and legs to his body, Ingram has the Sole Survivor assemble a series of [[electromagnetic actuator]]s using a [[Electromagnetic actuators list|list of materials]]. With those completed, the next hurdle is giving the robot an ample supply of [[Mark 28]] nuclear &amp;quot;footballs&amp;quot; to use; fortunately, she says Proctor Quinlan was able to find the coordinates of the [[Sentinel site|Sentinel Site Prescott]] bomb storage facility in the region, however it resides deep in the reaches of the [[Glowing Sea]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The Sole Survivor travels to the [[edge of the Glowing Sea]], where they meet up with Haylen and her armed escort at [[Waypoint Echo]]. She hands them a [[distress pulser]] that will help the recovery teams find the storage facility easier once they personally get inside, clear out any hostiles, and plant it in the Mark 28 bombs&amp;#039; location.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
After navigating the Glowing Sea and entering the upper floor, the Sole Survivor fights their way through the nesting feral ghouls and mole rats inside until reaching the loewr level where the bombs are kept. There, they encounter fallen members of the [[Church of the Children of Atom|Children of Atom]] as well as one living member, [[Henri|Brother Henri]] defended by the [[Assaultron (Fallout 4)|Assaultron]], [[Atom&amp;#039;s Wrath]]. Whether through violence or convincing him they are a follower of Atom themselves, the Sole Survivor unlocks the bomb storage room and plants the distress pulser. If Paladin Danse was brought as an active companion, he reveals his orders to remain behind and keep the bombs secure for the recovery teams.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Back at the airport, once confirmation of the nukes&amp;#039; acquisition reaches Ingram, she and the roboticist also confirm that their progress on modifying Prime&amp;#039;s power systems is complete. They give the opportunity to press the button to power him on to the knight. Once the robot finishes its diagnostic checks, Ingram informs the knight that Elder Maxson requested them for a meeting on the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039;.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Blind Betrayal]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Upon meeting Elder Maxson on the command deck, the Sole Survivor is immediately faced with hostility and accusations of withholding secrets. He reveals that the data from the network scanner included a list of escaped synths the Institute was looking for, among which was one, &amp;quot;M7-97&amp;quot; that was an exact match, both physically and genetically, for Paladin Danse. Worse still, when the Brotherhood attempted to find him, he had apparently disappeared from his post, fueling further suspicions. Though Maxson is soon convinced that the Sole Survivor themselves was unaware of this beforehand, he then charges them with leading the synth-hunt for Danse and executing him for his crimes.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
As the knight goes to discuss with Proctor Quinlan about ways to locate the missing Danse, they are interrupted by Haylen who initially castigates them for turning their backs on Danse, but upon being reprimanded for her insubordination by Quinlan, calms down and asks to meet with the knight privately. Out of earshot of the others, Haylen makes a genuine plea to them not to kill Danse, and regardless of their answer, she reveals that Danse set up a fallback location for their recon squad in case of emergencies, giving them the location of [[Listening Post Bravo]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
When they reach the isolated outpost and get past the active security turrets and [[Protectron (Fallout 4)|Protectron]] guards, the Sole Survivor confronts Danse. The former paladin confesses that he is no longer certain of who he is and knows that his attempt to isolate only made the situation worse. But he is still fully committed to the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s mission and bids them to carry out the orders they were given and go through with his execution.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
If they agree to the choice, Danse gives them some final thankful words and is executed, with his holo-tags brought back as proof of the deed. They can also refuse to do it and leave without another word, leading to Danse committing suicide instead.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
If they refuse to do it and tell Danse that he must live, he protests, stating synths are abominations and that he must be made an example of. But through persuasion, he is convinced to stay alive and flee the Commonwealth. However, when they leave the outpost, they find Elder Maxson personally waiting for them, having suspected the knight would defy their orders. Maxson lays out his beliefs in the necessity of destroying the Institute&amp;#039;s creations to prevent any chance of humanity&amp;#039;s eventual extinction, while Danse counters in reminding him of the pride he felt in serving the Brotherhood. Elder Maxson demands the knight fulfill their orders and execute Danse, or else he will.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
In addition to doing the deed themselves, if the Sole Survivor refuses but fails to convince Maxson to spare Danse, the Elder expresses his disappointment and uses his own knife and hands to finish off the traitor.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
If he is successfully persuaded to spare Danse, Maxson makes a final decree that he is letting the synth go free, but from then on considered an enemy of the Brotherhood to be killed on sight. Danse thanks the Sole Survivor for believing in him and giving him a chance to make something new of his life. Meanwhile, back on board the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039;, Maxson puts the subject of Danse to rest and decides to promote a new paladin to fill the vacancy, including personal quarters on the airship and Danse&amp;#039;s former set of T-60 power armor, then orders them to speak with Lancer-Captain Kells for their next assignment.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Tactical Thinking]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Upon reporting to the bridge on the command deck, the Sole Survivor is informed by Kells that the Brotherhood cannot have any loose ends when it comes to attacking the Institute, and so they must first deal with the Railroad. He claims that although they are not a major threat currently, their history and known tactics suggest the possibility that they will be, which means proactive measures must be taken and the Railroad&amp;#039;s leadership eradicated to ensure their complete destruction. He also tacks on an additional objective to acquire the Railroad&amp;#039;s advanced [[Artificial personality|AI]] collaborator, [[Predictive Analytic Machine|P.A.M.]] to put its probability calculating potential to use for the Brotherhood.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The Sole Survivor heads to the [[Old North Church]] and finds [[Railroad agent]] defenders trying to hinder their path, but they manage to fight through to the catacombs below. They see the Brotherhood had rigged the hidden entrance to the headquarters with explosives and loudly announces their arrival. Inside the heart of the headquarters, the Brotherhood paladin completes their mission by eliminating the five core members of Railroad leadership including [[Desdemona]], [[Stanley Carrington|Doctor Carrington]], [[Deacon]], [[Glory]], and [[Tinker Tom]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Once both objectives are dealt with, they return to the airport to debrief with Kells. If P.A.M. was successfully captured and her programmed allegiance rewritten, he commends them on their service to the Brotherhood. If she was instead simply destroyed, he expresses his disappointment that a valuable asset was denied to them but confirms they accomplished the primary objective.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Spoils of War]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Returning to Ingram on the tarmac, the Sole Survivor is told that Liberty Prime is nearly ready for deployment against the Institute, but will need one final component, a [[beryllium agitator]] in order to sustain his power systems for the oncoming battle. The only one the Brotherhood has been able to find in the region resides in the sub-level reactor of the [[Mass Fusion building]] downtown. The proctor herself also volunteers to accompany them on the retrieval mission, though she can also be persuaded to stay. Before boarding the [[Vertibird (Fallout 4)|vertibird]] to head to Mass Fusion, the Sole Survivor is informed that doing so will irrevocably destroy their alliance with the Institute and turn them hostile.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Upon arriving in the skies above the [[Mass Fusion executive suite|executive suite]], the Brotherhood paladin&amp;#039;s aircraft is fired upon by a sentry squad of synths and they are forced to land in the hot zone and clear them out. Though their intel suggested the agitator would be stored in the suite&amp;#039;s facility, the Sole Survivor discovers it was moved and they are forced to reactivate the elevator to descend into the building&amp;#039;s interior.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Partway down the elevator ride, the power is sabotaged and they are forced to disembark. The Sole Survivor can either restore the power to the elevator and finish the direct tide down or navigate through the synth-infested offices behind the main shaft in order to reach the lobby floor, where more synths reside, and get through the pre-War security to head down to the reactor level.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Outside the sealed room of the reactor containing the beryllium agitator core, the Sole Survivor can attempt to disable the suite of security robots including [[Assaultron (Fallout 4)|Assaultrons]] and a [[Sentry bot (Fallout 4)|sentry bot]] prior to removing it, otherwise they are activated immediately and attack. Due to the highly radioactive material of the agitator, the Sole Survivor is forced to use anti-rad measures such as [[Rad-X (Fallout 4)|Rad-X]] and/or a [[Hazmat suit (Fallout 4)|hazmat suit]] to safely remove it from its apparatus.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
When returning to the lobby where the exit lies, the Sole Survivor encounters a ground battle between a new wave of synths and Brotherhood defenders that were deployed to back them up. They can either flee the battlefield to complete their mission or assists their brothers- and sisters-in-arms to finish off the enemy wave before heading back to the airport.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Ad Victoriam]]====&lt;br /&gt;
With the beryllium agitator secure in the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s hands, once Ingram sees it for herself, she offers the honor of putting in the final piece of the puzzle on Liberty Prime to the paladin who made it possible. The Sole Survivor implants the device into the robotic super weapon and hits the final button. With his routines fully online and itching to destroy communists, Prime begins the long march across the ruined Boston in the direction of the C.I.T. ruins and the Institute below it, with fully Brotherhood offensive support in tow.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Apart from a temporary stop during his patriotic subroutine to salute the [[Bunker Hill]] monument, Prime walks unopposed to C.I.T., wrecking synth interceptors, raider and super mutant nuisances, and even steamrolling through one or two behemoths that try to cross him. On the site of the old university campus, the biggest wave of synths are teleported in, so too does the Brotherhood deploy its biggest assault force, including Elder Maxson himself entering the fray. After a momentary scan for his target, Prime unleashes his powerful eye laser to carve a hole through the rocky exterior of the Institute, opening the way for the Brotherhood to enter the beast&amp;#039;s belly.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[The Nuclear Option (Brotherhood of Steel)|The Nuclear Option]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Once through the hole carved by Liberty Prime, placing them near the Relay chamber, Elder Maxson gives the fusion pulse charge they will need to destroy the Institute&amp;#039;s nuclear factor and ensure its complete destruction, while in the meantime Ingram works on getting the teleporter ready for their exit.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The Brotherhood&amp;#039;s advance through the main concourse meets an Institute defensive of armored synths and coursers but it proves fruitless as they head on to Advanced Systems, only to find the entrance locked down and the only option to undo it in the director&amp;#039;s quarters. There, the Sole Survivor finds Shaun, dying of a terminal illness but remaining wholly convinced the Institute would have been the solution to humanity&amp;#039;s problems. Through theft of it or persuading their elderly son to help them just once, the Sole Survivor undoes the lockdown and potentially also issues an all-hands evacuation order for the entire facility.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
In the reactor room, the Brotherhood faces the Institute&amp;#039;s remaining enforcers trying to prevent their destructive ambitions but it does not last, and the Sole Survivor plants the charge inside the volatile machinery. Ingram briefly brings the strike force back to the Relay room, where the Sole Survivor discovers the child Shaun synth waiting for them and now fully believing it is their son. Once a decision is made about the synth, Ingram is given the go-ahead to teleport everyone out for the last time. They are then deposited on the top floor of the Mass Fusion building, looking out in the direction of Cambridge and the C.I.T. ruins. With the push of a pre-set detonator, they witness the nuclear explosion engulf the area and cementing the Institute&amp;#039;s destruction once and for all. The Sole Survivor has a momentary reflection on their journey before conferring with Elder Maxson, who congratulates them on securing the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s victory and removing the Institute&amp;#039;s technological threat from the Commonwealth. He bids them to return to the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039; when they have time, as there are plans in place for moving forward.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[A New Dawn]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Upon reporting back to the airship, the Sole Survivor finds Elder Maxson and Lancer-Captain Kells waiting for them on the command deck. They celebrate the victory over the Institute and commend them for helping cement the Commonwealth&amp;#039;s bright future. Maxson then bestows upon them the esteemed rank of sentinel, with free reign to do as they please including whether they wish to continue assisting the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s interests.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===The Railroad===&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Road to Freedom]]====&lt;br /&gt;
In addition to encountering them while trying to get the courser chip decoded, the Sole Survivor can also hear about the rumor to &amp;quot;follow the Freedom Trail&amp;quot; from [[Resident (Diamond City)|residents]] of [[Diamond City]] during their first visit there. In this circumstance, following the Trail into the Old North Church and decoding the password leads to a different conversation where [[Deacon]] vouches for them by citing previous feats of theirs (or their extraordinary abilities if they have on feats), with Desdemona then questioning them on the matter of saving synth lives, with positive answers earning her interest, though not yet her full trust.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Tradecraft]]====&lt;br /&gt;
The Sole Survivor is then taken aside by Deacon who tries to reassure them that he is on the side of getting them into the Railroad&amp;#039;s ranks and has a plan to speed that process along, involving breaking into the group&amp;#039;s former headquarters, [[the Switchboard]] beneath a [[Slocum&amp;#039;s Joe (Fallout 4)|Slocum&amp;#039;s Joe]] outlet in [[Lexington]] to retrieve [[Carrington&amp;#039;s prototype|a piecei of technology]]. He is certain this will impress Desdemona enough to bring them into the fold in short order and requests they rendezvous outside the city.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Following Deacon&amp;#039;s instructions, they go to meet him where he gets to show off his knack for disguises and blending in. He says they have to get some intelligence on the situation in Lexington before making a plan and informs them about the Railroad&amp;#039;s concept of &amp;quot;tourists,&amp;quot; providing outside assistance without being too involved as to be vulnerable to the Institute. While ascending a broken section of overpass to the tourist&amp;#039;s lookout, Deacon explains a little about the Railroad&amp;#039;s use of [[railsign]]s as a covert language their operatives can recognize anywhere. From the tourist, [[Ricky Dalton]], they learn that the coffee shop exterior is littered with synths in addition to an extensive [[Fragmentation mine (Fallout 4)|minefield]] on the northern road. Deacon suggests using an old escape tunnel into a sewer system connected to the subterranean facility comprising the Switchboard. Though he also offers the option to commit a frontal assault through the synths and mines, it will end up that the secret entrance is locked from the inside, forcing going through the escape tunnel anyway.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Inside the escape tunnel, Deacon holds them up to expand some more on the Railroad&amp;#039;s mission, including an ongoing background schism on whether to rescue just the free-willed Gen 3 synths or both them and the mechanical, programmed Gen 1 and 2 synths. He uses old passcodes to get them through the security lockdown and traverse the old sewer system, fighting through the defensive contingent of synths left behind to guard the facility. Upon getting into the sealed vault containing their objective, Deacon finds the body of a former comrade, [[Tommy Whispers]] and offers the Sole Survivor the ownership of Tommy&amp;#039;s [[Deliverer|personal sidearm]] as a token of good faith. With the prototype in hand, they make their escape from the Switchboard and split up to more easily make it back to the HQ unnoticed.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Returning just after Deacon, the Sole Survivor finds him already trying to spin a tall tale of heroism to Desdemona, and they can either go along with it or directly tell the truth. Either way, Desdemona is impressed by their initiative and this finally tips her over to accept them as a ranking member of the Railroad privy to their ongoing field operations. This includes their choice of codename, though even if they decline, she says it is mandatory in their organization and gives them one herself.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Underground Undercover]]====&lt;br /&gt;
During the construction of the signal interceptor at [[Mercer Safehouse]], the Sole Survivor is given an additional task by Desdemona on top of Tinker Tom&amp;#039;s request involving the network scanner. She orders them to locate a mysterious ally inside the Institute known as &amp;quot;Patriot,&amp;quot; who has been vital to the Railroad&amp;#039;s synth-liberating in the past but that nobody really knows.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Inside the Institute, the Sole Survivor sends a coded message through the computer system covertly and receives a reply from Patriot to meet in a discreet area. Upon going there, they discover the identity of Patriot as [[Liam Binet]], son of [[Institute Robotics|Robotics division]] head [[Alan Binet]]. Liam expresses surprise that anyone decoded the messages he was sending out with each synth he freed and were then able to contact him back, and he is excited at the prospect of coordinating with the Railroad.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Liam takes them to meet with an accomplice of his, synth worker [[Z1-14]], who reveals that they have been recently organizing an escape attempt for a group of thirteen synths. To escape, Liam says he will need to bypass SRB electronic security to open some doors, but he is not able to crack it. Instead, he requests a pre-War admin password from the surface, recommending asking the Railroad for any leads on where to find one.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Back at the Railroad HQ, with the help of P.A.M.&amp;#039;s calculations, the Sole Survivor is able to track down the login credentials formerly held by [[Wilfred Bergman]], the director of the [[Cambridge Polymer Labs, LLC|Cambridge Polymer Labs]]. However, upon returning from there with the credentials, they are halted by Desdemona, who confides her concerns about Liam&amp;#039;s plans. She concludes that if it is successful, it may never be possible for Liam to free another synth ever again due to the Institute going on high-alert following such a security breach. She proposes instead that they should use this opportunity to free every synth in the Institute; to do that would require the synths themselves to rise up and fight for their freedom, instead of operating in the shadows. She instructs the Sole Survivor to give Liam the password he requested, but then to cut him loose and coordinate with Z1-14 instead from then on.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Back in the Institute, the Sole Survivor gives Liam the password, then meets with Z1-14 to discuss Desdemona&amp;#039;s proposal. He agrees with the plan and asks for 24 hours to ensure that it is possible. Once time elapses, they reconvene with Z1-14 expressing surprise at how many other synths he had gotten on board with the plan. The scale of it will require them to arm themselves in the event of violence, so he requests that the Sole Survivor aid them by killing a few guards so they can stage a construction accident, allowing a few synths to go into hiding in order to construct weapons. The Sole Survivor may optionally offer to provide additional weapons, which Z1-14 instructs to be placed in a maintenance closet.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
After the guards overseeing the allied synths are dispatched, the worker synths give their thanks and collapse the tunnel after the Sole Survivor departs. Speaking with Z1-14, he gives his thanks as well and states that they need time to prepare, in the meantime, they should maintain their cover by continuing to work with Father and the Institute until they are ready.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Precipice of War]]====&lt;br /&gt;
