Shortly after [[Powering Up (Fallout 4)|reactivating the Institute&amp;#039;s nuclear reactor]] and hearing about their plans to eventually deal with both the [[Brotherhood of Steel (Fallout 4)|Brotherhood]] and the Railroad, the Sole Survivor is found by the synth [[R5]] who gives them a covert message to meet Z1 in their personal quarters, publicly to deal with a flooding issue there. In private, he reveals he came across communications indicating the Brotherhood had discovered the location of the Railroad&amp;#039;s base and were preparing an assault to eradicate them.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The Sole Survivor arrives to inform Desdemona of the attack shortly before it commences and the Railroad is forced on the defensive as Brotherhood soldiers invade through their escape route. After the first wave is repelled, Desdemona reveals [[Glory]] had gone ahead into the catacombs to secure the exit that way and they have to catch up. However, they arrive too late and though Glory was able to push the Brotherhood back, she was dealt serious wounds with only moments for some final words before passing away. The Sole Survivor takes the lead in fighting the remainder of the Brotherhood assault force all the way to the main floor of the church.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
However, upon reconvening with Desdemona, she surmises the Brotherhood won&amp;#039;t stop their attack and will likely send stronger forces until they finish the job. Thus, she enacts Operation Red Glare and orders them to lead a strike against the outpost at the [[Cambridge Police Station]] and secure the means to reach [[The Prydwen|the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039;]] and end the Brotherhood for good.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Upon meeting with Tinker Tom and Deacon on the outskirts of the Police Station, the techie points out their objective of securing the vertibird [[Claymore (vertibird)|&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Claymore&amp;#039;&amp;#039;]] to get them onto the airship. The Sole Survivor then assaults the Police Station, eliminating all standing Brotherhood personnel including Scribe Haylen and Knight Rhys to give Tom an opening to come in and get the vertibird ready for takeoff. They are briefly interrupted by the arrival of a second vertibird, [[Scimitar (vertibird)|&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Scimitar&amp;#039;&amp;#039;]] but manage to take down its crew, giving them room to get ready before heading for the [[Boston Airport]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Rockets&amp;#039; Red Glare]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Just before takeoff in their hijacked vertibird, Deacon supplies the Sole Survivor with some [[Explosive charge (Fallout 4)|explosive charges]] for the plan to rig them along the gas-filled tanks keeping the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039; afloat, intending to ignite them and blow it up to cripple the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s leadership. With the go-ahead, the trio takes to the skies and flies in the direction of the airport.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
If they never interacted with the Brotherhood beforehand, Deacon gives the Sole Survivor a Brotherhood uniform to carry out an infiltration, allowing them to move practically invisible through the airship carrying out their sabotage.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
If they forgo Deacon&amp;#039;s plan, or already are hostile to the Brotherhood, the airship&amp;#039;s crew puts up a fierce fight to defend against the invaders, requiring the charges to be planted under laser-fire.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
However they manage to get the charges in place, the saboteur crew quickly hops back onto their stolen vertibird to flee the airport, followed by Tom executing detonation as they witness the flying fortress&amp;#039; flaming remains crashing onto the airport tarmac below. Tom then has his passengers hop off nearby so he can take the aircraft to a secure stash location for later tinkering.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Back at the Railroad HQ, Desdemona congratulates them on toppling one powerful enemy and determines that the time has come to repeat the feat: the attack on the Institute starts soon, once they get the all-clear from Z1 on the inside.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[The Nuclear Option (Railroad)|The Nuclear Option]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Once inside and meeting up with Z1, he request them to clear out any personnel in the Relay chamber so that he can use it to bring in the Railroad&amp;#039;s assault force. The moment that Desdemona, Tinker Tom, and several Railroad agents arrive, the Institute goes into lockdown, forcing them to utilize the old Robotics wing connected to the Relay chamber to get around it. Once they re-enter the concourse, they find Z1&amp;#039;s rebel synths in the middle of their attack and the Railroad obliges to join in.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
After Tom figures out that the lockdown cutting them off from the Advanced Systems room can be undone on the director&amp;#039;s terminal, the Sole Survivor goes up to meet the bed-ridden Shaun, in the final stages of his terminal cancer diagnosis. He rebukes them for throwing away the bright future the Institute could have brought to the Commonwealth, but can be persuaded to allow them to save some of his people&amp;#039;s lives by issuing the evacuation order from his terminal.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Upon getting into the reactor room from Advanced Systems and fighting through the remaining defenders, the Sole Survivor plants the fusion pulse charge Tom gave them onto the Institute&amp;#039;s reactor, and is then teleported to the Relay chamber. They find there that the synth Shaun was reprogrammed to believe they are his parent and wants to go with them; even if the Sole Survivor refuses, Tinker Tom still takes the time to coordinate Shaun&amp;#039;s removal to a safe location along with the Railroad themselves. They are then deposited on the top floor of the [[Mass Fusion building]], looking out in the direction of Cambridge and the C.I.T. ruins. With the push of a pre-set detonator, they witness the nuclear explosion engulf the area and cementing the Institute&amp;#039;s destruction once and for all. The Sole Survivor has a momentary reflection on their journey before conferring with Desdemona, who once more congratulates them on saving the Commonwealth from its greatest enemy and remarks that though there will be retaliation from the Institute forces that managed to escape, they will never recover from the destruction of their HQ and research. She however admits that even with the Institute no longer stalking in the shadows, the people of the Commonwealth will never shed their fear of synthkind, but they will always have an ally in the Railroad.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===The Institute===&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Synth Retention]]====&lt;br /&gt;
After completing their tour of the Institute and receiving his good faith gift via Madison Li in Advanced Systems, the Sole Survivor returns to speak with Father, who expresses his hope that they will eventually shed any doubts about the value of the organization in improving the Commonwealth. He then suggests a way for them to better prove their allegiance and reveals that the Institute discovered a runaway synth, under the name [[Gabriel (Fallout 4)|Gabriel]] after getting a memory wipe from [[the Railroad]], has taken over the [[Raiders (Fallout 4)|raider]] gang occupying [[Libertalia]] and led them on many bloody escapades; in response, a [[courser]] has been dispatched to retrieve the synth and Father wants them to meet up at the [[Nahant Wharf]] and help out.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Traveling to [[Nahant]], the Sole Survivor finds the courser sent on the mission, [[X6-88]] and they storm the raider stronghold together. Eventually, they advanced up the downturned freighter serving as the main structure and reach the captain&amp;#039;s cabin, shortly before the roof of the structure where Gabriel and his remaining men had assembled. X6 halts them momentarily to give them the [[recall code]] for Gabriel, to be used to immediately incapacitate him for retrieval back to the Institute. When encountering Gabriel, the Sole Survivor can either issue the recall code and let X6 take him back to the Institute, or forgo it and instead kill Gabriel themselves.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Back in the Institute, Father gives his assessment of their actions at Libertalia, expressing disappointment if they elected to kill Gabriel and deny the Institute one of its synths. Regardless, he still intends to move forward with their cooperation and issues the Sole Survivor their own personal quarters above the facility&amp;#039;s concourse. While exploring their new lodgings, X6 appears to inform them that Father asked they meet with him again once they were settled in.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[The Battle of Bunker Hill]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Returning to the director&amp;#039;s quarters, the Sole Survivor is posed a question by him on the matter of reclaiming something that was stolen, which transitions into a discussion on taking retaliatory measures against the Railroad&amp;#039;s interference in their operations; he wants to start with a direct strike against a safehouse within the walls of the [[Bunker Hill]] trading hub, where they currently house a group of escaped synths. Father requests they take the lead in recovering them and do so with haste, before either the Railroad or the Brotherhood of Steel can take action that interferes with that goal.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Despite the Institute&amp;#039;s best efforts, when the Sole Survivor reaches the outskirts of Bunker Hill and meets with their [[X4-18|courser contact]], he reveals that the Railroad has already formed a defensive line within the settlement and the Brotherhood has sent vertibird attack squads to break it. With the go-ahead, they charge into the chaotic three-way battle as synth reinforcements are teleported in. After skirting around the perimeter to find a side entrance, the Sole Survivor reaches the undercroft where the synths are being sheltered in an isolated room. From here, the Sole Survivor can either let the courser proceed with issuing the recall codes and retrieving the synths, betray and kill the courser to save the synths, or kill the synths themselves and inviting the courser&amp;#039;s hostile response.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Whatever the outcome, once they leave Bunker Hill, the Sole Survivor goes to the C.I.T. ruins and onto its rooftop, where they find Father looking out upon the Commonwealth. He speaks wistfully on the state of the surface and how it only further confirms for himself that he was right to put his faith in the Institute&amp;#039;s future. Father then changes the subject to the outcome at Bunker Hill, expressing disappointment if the synths were not recovered for either reason, but accepts it if they express regret over the failure of the mission. He explains that soon the directorate will be meeting to discuss important plans ahead and he wants his parent to attend as they will also be involved.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Mankind - Redefined]]====&lt;br /&gt;
The Sole Survivor follows Father&amp;#039;s invitation and goes to the meeting room where him and the various division heads of the Institute have assembled. To start, an assessment of surface activity and the movements of the Commonwealth&amp;#039;s major powers is given by [[Justin Ayo]]. Father tries to approach the subject of &amp;quot;Phase Three,&amp;quot; but the Advanced Systems leader hesitates due to the Sole Survivor&amp;#039;s presence, so he explains himself that the Institute desires to bring a powerful nuclear reactor online to generate unlimited power and plans are in motion to solve that problem, of which he wants them to be directly involved. He then moves to a more difficult subject, revealing to everyone, his parent included, that he is dying from terminal [[cancer]] that none of the Institute&amp;#039;s medical procedures have been able to prevent. It is for that reason that he immediately announces that he will elect his parent as the new director of the Institute, telling the objecting division heads that their organization needs a leader more than a scientist, someone who truly knows what is best for the wasteland.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
With the meeting adjourned and after the division heads leave, parent and son have a private conversation where Shaun apologizes for leaving them unprepared to receive those two impactful pieces of information in such a short time. He expresses that it is unavoidable that there will be some who disapprove of his choice to put them in charge, but he hopes they will prove their doubters wrong. He advises they take the opportunity to prove their loyalty to the Institute by speaking to the Facilities division head, [[Allie Filmore]] and spearheading the next stage of Phase Three.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Mass Fusion (quest)|Mass Fusion]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Once they find Allie Filmore again, the Sole Survivor is brought in on the plan to bring the reactor online, which Allie reveals will require a piece of technology capable of achieving the reaction they need: the [[beryllium agitator]] being kept at the [[Mass Fusion building]]. However, scouts have reported the presence of the Brotherhood of Steel on-site, so she wants to get there quickly before they take the agitator for themselves, as well as volunteering to go with them personally; she promises not to get in the way but does not totally trust the Sole Survivor with the delicate technology. She can react disappointedly if told she will be more of a liability and left behind. Before entering the Molecular Relay to head to Mass Fusion, the Sole Survivor is informed that doing so will irrevocably destroy their alliance with the Brotherhood and turn them hostile.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The Relay deposits the surface party on the top-floor [[Mass Fusion executive suite|executive suite]], where they immediately face the defending group of Brotherhood soldiers. When they try to pick up the agitator from the suite&amp;#039;s safe, the Sole Survivor finds it is missing which means the only other place it could be is in the subterranean reactor room. They power on the elevator to descend inside the building. However, shortly into their descent, the Brotherhood cuts the power to the elevator, forcing them to disembark. Once they either locate the necessary pre-War credentials in the outer offices, or manually reset the system, they can restore the elevator&amp;#039;s power. Finally getting to the lobby, they head through the access door to the reactor sublevel.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Outside the sealed room of the reactor containing the beryllium agitator core, the Sole Survivor can attempt to disable the suite of security robots including [[Assaultron (Fallout 4)|Assaultrons]] and a [[Sentry bot (Fallout 4)|sentry bot]] prior to removing it, otherwise they are activated immediately and attack. Due to the highly radioactive material of the agitator, the Sole Survivor is forced to use anti-rad measures such as [[Rad-X (Fallout 4)|Rad-X]] and/or a [[Hazmat suit (Fallout 4)|hazmat suit]] to safely remove it from its apparatus.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
When returning to the lobby where the exit lies, the Sole Survivor encounters a ground battle between a new wave of Brotherhood defenders along with synth reinforcements that were deployed to back them up. They can either flee the battlefield to complete their mission or assists the mechanical soldiers to finish off the enemy wave before teleporting back to the Institute.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Pinned]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Upon returning and handing over the beryllium agitator to Dr. Filmore, she advises that Father had requested to talk to them about another important matter. The Sole Survivor goes to find him in [[Dean Volkert]]&amp;#039;s clinic conversing on his healthcare, but stops the conversation when they arrive. Shaun commends them for getting the agitator and asks them to help the Institute with a matter of recruitment, namely assisting a diplomatic mission that was trying to bring in a wasteland researcher, [[T.S. Wallace]], however, he was spooked by the appearance of their synth bodyguards, barricaded himself in [[Graygarden homestead|a shelter]], and requested help from an armed force of [[Gunners]] (or [[Minuteman|Minutemen]] if they were helped to [[#Taking Independence|retake the Castle]]). Father wants him brought back alive, no matter the cost.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
At the old homestead outside [[Graygarden]], the Sole Survivor has to deal with Wallace&amp;#039;s armed backup. While the Gunners will attack regardless and must be eliminated, the Minutemen will be surprised to see their general there and they have three options to resolve the situation: killing the Minutemen which will turn their faction hostile; siding with the Minutemen and betraying the Institute which will turn their faction hostile; or successfully persuading the Minutemen to back down and let the Institute complete their mission.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Inside the shelter, the Sole Survivor finds [[Enrico Thompson]] who requests their help convincing Wallace to comply. From behind a locked door, Wallace can either be nicely persuaded or harshly threatened; should that fail, Thompson takes an alternate option of having a synth teleport into the room and knock the scientist unconscious to take him back by force.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Powering Up (Fallout 4)|Powering Up]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Once they report back to Father, he congratulates them on adding onto the Institute&amp;#039;s successes and requests they assist with some of the final steps of Phase Three. Firstly, he has his parent construct an [[announcement script]] that will be used to declare the Institute&amp;#039;s public emergence on the surface&amp;#039;s political stage, then sends them to [[Diamond City]] to boost the range of the radio transmitter for [[Diamond City Radio]] so that their message will reach the whole Commonwealth.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Back in the Institute, the Sole Survivor meets with Shaun in the chamber containing the Institute&amp;#039;s nuclear reactor, where he gives them the honor of plugging in the beryllium agitator to complete Phase Three and bring the reactor online. He thanks them for all they have done so far and then invites them to sit in at their first meeting as director and get to know the procedures of their organization. At the meeting, they are filled in by the directorate on the progress of plans to deal with both the Brotherhood of Steel and the Railroad, as well as Allie Filmore asking for their advice on where to focus new development projects now that the power situation has been remedied.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[End of the Line]]====&lt;br /&gt;
Returning to speak with Shaun, he asks the Sole Survivor&amp;#039;s opinion on the Institute&amp;#039;s plans for taking down the Brotherhood of Steel; even if they respond that it is a mistake to do so, Father will insist that it is the only right decision because otherwise the Institute will be destroyed. He then asks whether they are ready to turn on the Railroad. Even if they say that they are allies and argues to save the synth-liberators, Shaun insists that they must destroy the Railroad and says it is an order, not a request.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Upon finding Desdemona, if the Railroad was never interacted with after decoding the courser chip, she says that the Railroad is aware that the Sole Survivor is working with the Institute and asks them to leave, with the rest of the Railroad being dismissive of them. After killing the leaders of the Railroad including Desdemona, Dr. Carrington, Tinker Tom, Deacon, and Glory, effectively crippling the organization, they return to Father in the Institute. He tells them about moving forward with the plan to drive the Brotherhood of Steel out from the Commonwealth, teaching them that the Institute can, at any time and place, turn their own technology against them. They are instructed to speak with Doctor Li about getting started.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Airship Down]]====&lt;br /&gt;
At Advanced Systems, the Sole Survivor speaks with Doctor Li who then directs them to her colleague [[Rosalind Orman]] for the information on defeating the Brotherhood. Orman explains that so far, the Institute has been unable to send in synth attackers at Boston Airport due to an electromagnetic field scattering the Relay&amp;#039;s signal. However, they have learned this field is supported by a series of generators that, once they can get in and destroy them, will open the way for an attack force to support the Sole Survivor in their main objective: getting a hacking virus into the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s superweapon, [[Liberty Prime]] that will cause it to turn its powerful weapons against them and destroy [[The Prydwen|the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Prydwen&amp;#039;&amp;#039;]], thus dismantling the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s power base in the Commonwealth.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
Once teleported as close to the airport as they can get, the Sole Survivor breaches the Brotherhood&amp;#039;s defenses and systematically destroys the EM generators, allowing waves of synths to be brought in to fight with them against the Brotherhood. Once the last generator falls, they escort a [[Synth virus|Gen 1]] loaded with the hacking virus to the top of the rampart supporting the pre-War robot, then defends it against enemy attackers, including [[Arthur Maxson|Elder Maxson]] himself descending to the battlefield in full power armor. The fight ultimately ends in the Institute&amp;#039;s favor, as Prime is reprogrammed to see the airship as a [[Communism|communist]] vessel and annihilates it with his eye laser, with the flaming remains crashing onto the airport tarmac and destroying everything though the Sole Survivor is teleported to a safe distance beforehand.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
====[[Nuclear Family]]====&lt;br /&gt;
After returning to the Institute for the final time, the Sole Survivor finds the bedridden Shaun, in the final stages of his terminal cancer, but happy to see that he was able to achieve a bright future for the Institute by passing the leadership to his parent. The Sole Survivor has a momentary reflection on their journey as Shaun passes away, with some time passing by which time the Institute has erected a memorial to him in the concourse.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

		<item>
			<title>Upcoming Obsidian Fallout</title>
			<link>https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Upcoming_Obsidian_Fallout</link>
			<guid isPermaLink="false">https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Upcoming_Obsidian_Fallout</guid>
			<description>&lt;p&gt;FDSMDP: Wasn&amp;#039;t Avellone talking about a different game? At least, we don&amp;#039;t know that these are the same one afaik&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr /&gt;
&lt;div&gt;{{Upcoming content}}&lt;br /&gt;
{{Infobox game&lt;br /&gt;
|image        =&lt;br /&gt;
|developer    =[[Obsidian Entertainment]]&amp;lt;br /&amp;gt;[[Bethesda Game Studios]]&lt;br /&gt;
|engine       =Unknown&lt;br /&gt;
|genre        =Role-playing game&lt;br /&gt;
|media        =&lt;br /&gt;
|modes        =Single player&lt;br /&gt;
|platforms    =&lt;br /&gt;
|publisher    =[[Bethesda Softworks]]&lt;br /&gt;
|rating       =&lt;br /&gt;
|release date =Unknown&lt;br /&gt;
|requirements =&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;upcoming Obsidian &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Fallout&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is an unannounced, upcoming title in the [[Fallout series|&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Fallout&amp;#039;&amp;#039; series]], developed by [[Obsidian Entertainment]]. [[Bethesda Game Studios]] is also purported to work on the project. No official statement has been made from any involved parties regarding the title&amp;#039;s existence, though it was reported on by Bloomberg News in July 2026.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Overview==&lt;br /&gt;
In July 2026, following layoffs and structural changes at Microsoft&amp;#039;s Xbox division, journalist Jason Schreier of Bloomberg News reported that plans were being set in motion for Obsidian Entertainment to work on the development of a new &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Fallout&amp;#039;&amp;#039; game. The game would be under the direction of [[Joshua Sawyer]], who was the project director on &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Fallout: New Vegas&amp;#039;&amp;#039; (2010). Bethesda Game Studios&amp;#039; Rockville division would also work with Obsidian on the new project. Schreier reported that the plans were an &amp;quot;emerging strategy&amp;quot; that was &amp;quot;still in flux&amp;quot; and could still change.&amp;lt;ref&amp;gt;{{cite web |url=https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-07-08/microsoft-s-xbox-to-shift-obsidian-studio-to-new-fallout-video-game?accessToken=eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJzb3VyY2UiOiJTdWJzY3JpYmVyR2lmdGVkQXJ0aWNsZSIsImlhdCI6MTc4MzUzMzE2NSwiZXhwIjoxNzg0MTM3OTY1LCJhcnRpY2xlSWQiOiJUSFZBWklWVFRDWk0wMCIsImJjb25uZWN0SWQiOiJCMUVBQkI5NjQ2QUM0REZFQTJBRkI4MjI1MzgyQTJFQSJ9.9UWhy-Whti5Meu89-fQ5LQd_ZHqAtJVbXoWOcVtFkkU&amp;amp;leadSource=uverify%20wall|title=Microsoft’s Xbox to Shift Obsidian Studio to New ‘Fallout’ Video Game|accessdate=2026-07-08 |last=Shreier|first=Jason|date=2026-07-08|publisher=Bloomberg|language=English|archiveurl=https://archive.ph/FYJjs|archivedate=07-08-2026}}&amp;lt;/ref&amp;gt;&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==See also==&lt;br /&gt;
* &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Fallout 5]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Fallout 3 remaster|&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Fallout 3&amp;#039;&amp;#039; remaster]]&lt;br /&gt;

&lt;br /&gt;
The &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Rusted paint&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a [[Fallout 76 paint schemes|paint scheme]] {{in|FO76BP}} and expanded upon in the [[Milepost Zero (update)|Milepost Zero]] [[Fallout 76 updates|update]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
A gray paint scheme with spots and specks of rust on it.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Available on==&lt;br /&gt;
===Weapons===&lt;br /&gt;
* [[10mm pistol (Fallout 76)|10mm pistol]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Baseball bat (Fallout 76)|Baseball bat]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Combat rifle (Fallout 76)|Combat rifle]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Handmade rifle (Fallout 76)|Handmade rifle]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Hunting rifle (Fallout 76)|Hunting rifle]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Minigun (Fallout 76)|Minigun]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Pump action shotgun (Fallout 76)|Pump action shotgun]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Tesla rifle (Fallout 76)|Tesla rifle]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Western revolver (Fallout 76)|Western revolver]]&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===Power armor===&lt;br /&gt;
* [[T-45 power armor (Fallout 76)|T-45]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[T-51 power armor (Fallout 76)|T-51]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[T-60 power armor (Fallout 76)|T-60]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[T-65 power armor|T-65]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[X-01 power armor (Fallout 76)|X-01]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Excavator power armor|Excavator]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Hellcat power armor|Hellcat]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Ultracite power armor|Ultracite]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Union power armor|Union]]&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Location==&lt;br /&gt;
Most paints were obtained through the [[Milepost Zero: Country Road]] [[Fallout 76 seasons|season]], and afterwards in the [[Atomic Shop]], with the exception of the [[10mm pistol (Fallout 76)|10mm pistol]], [[Baseball bat (Fallout 76)|baseball bat]], [[Combat rifle (Fallout 76)|combat rifle]], [[Pump action shotgun (Fallout 76)|pump action shotgun]], and [[Western revolver (Fallout 76)|western revolver]] that are unlocked by [[Plan: Rusted weapon loadout paints]]. The plan is sold by [[June Seaver]] at [[Gilman lumber mill]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Irradiated paint&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a [[Fallout 76 paint schemes|paint scheme]] {{In|FO76GW}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
A black-ish paint scheme with light green cracks in it.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Available on==&lt;br /&gt;
===Weapons===&lt;br /&gt;
* [[.50 cal machine gun]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Auto axe (Fallout 76)|Auto axe]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Fat Man (Fallout 76)|Fat man]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Flamer (Fallout 76)|Flamer]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Gatling gun]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Railway rifle (Fallout 76)|Railway rifle]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[V63 laser carbine]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Western revolver (Fallout 76)|Western revolver]]&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Location==&lt;br /&gt;
* All weapon paints were obtained through the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Glow of the Ghoul (season)|Glow of the Ghoul]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039; [[Fallout 76 seasons|season]].&lt;br /&gt;

			<title>Santa paint</title>

			<guid isPermaLink="false">https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Santa_paint</guid>
			<description>&lt;p&gt;Jspoelstra: &lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr /&gt;
&lt;div&gt;{{Infobox incomplete|FO4CC}}&lt;br /&gt;
{{Infobox item&lt;br /&gt;
|games     =FO4CC&lt;br /&gt;
|type      =paint&lt;br /&gt;
|icon      =&lt;br /&gt;
|image     =Fo4CC HWP Santa paint combat armor.jpg&lt;br /&gt;

&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Santa paint&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a [[Creation Club paint schemes|paint scheme]] in the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Fallout 4]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039; [[Creation Club]] content &amp;quot;Holiday Workshop Pack.&amp;quot;&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
A shiny metallic red base paint with white lining and snowflakes along the edges.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Available on==&lt;br /&gt;
===Armor===&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Combat armor (Fallout 4)|Combat armor]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Leather armor (Fallout 4)|Leather armor]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Metal armor (Fallout 4)|Metal armor]]&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Synth armor]]&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Location==&lt;br /&gt;
Craftable at an [[Armor workbench (Fallout 4)|armor workbench]] after completing [[Holiday Spirits]], having picked up [[Picket Fences]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

&lt;br /&gt;
&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Granny Dolores&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a hostile bounty hunting target, found in the [[Burning Springs (region)|Burning Springs]] region of [[Appalachia]] {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
Granny Dolores is an elderly woman accompanied by her posse members, the [[enraged old lady|enraged old ladies]]. She has earned a bounty placed on her head for some misdeeds. She is wanted dead, and is one of several potential targets of [[the Ghoul]] and the [[Vault Dweller (Fallout 76)|Vault 76 dwellers]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
Dolores wields a [[Double-barrel shotgun (Fallout 76)|double-barrel shotgun]] with a +500% damage bonus multiplier, as well as the [[Two Shot Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Two Shot]], [[Last Shot Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Last Shot]], and [[Swift Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Swift]] [[Fallout 76 legendary weapon effects|legendary weapon effects]]. Additionally, Dolores will make nearby NPCs into her allies.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Interactions with the player character==&lt;br /&gt;
===Interactions overview===&lt;br /&gt;
{{Interactions FO76&lt;br /&gt;
|events involved    =[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]]&lt;br /&gt;
|quests involved    =&lt;br /&gt;
|killable           =yes&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===Quests===&lt;br /&gt;
[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]] - Granny Dolores is one of the potential targets to kill during this bounty hunting [[Fallout 76 public events|public event]]. She will be protected by enraged old ladies and [[Rust raider]]s. She is included as part of Group 6 for [[Fallout 76 challenges|challenges]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

&lt;br /&gt;
==Appearances==&lt;br /&gt;
Granny Dolores appears only {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Behind the scenes==&lt;br /&gt;
Granny Dolores and the enraged old ladies being a hostile encounter in Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt is [[Fallout 76 cultural references#Fallout: New Vegas|reminiscent of]] the [[Maud&amp;#039;s Muggers]] [[Wild Wasteland]] encounter in &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Fallout: New Vegas]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039;, which also featured a gang of hostile old women. Maud&amp;#039;s Muggers itself was a reference to the [[wikipedia:Monty Python|Monty Python]] skit &amp;quot;Hell&amp;#039;s Grannies.&amp;quot;&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;The Chief Researcher&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a hostile bounty hunting target, found in the [[Burning Springs (region)|Burning Springs]] region of [[Appalachia]] {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
The Chief Researcher is a hostile scientist wearing [[Arktos Pharma (company)|Arktos Pharma]] and [[Vault-Tec Corporation]] attire who appears to prefer using laser weapons, and is accompanied by [[science school dropout]]s. He has earned a bounty placed on his head for some misdeeds. He is wanted dead, and is one of several potential targets of [[the Ghoul]] and the [[Vault Dweller (Fallout 76)|Vault 76 dwellers]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
The Chief Researcher wields a [[Laser gun (Fallout 76)|laser gun]] that deals [[radiation]] damage and has a +300% damage bonus multiplier. He also has the &amp;quot;Anti-Mutant&amp;quot; perk, which deals 1.1x - 1.5x damage to [[Fallout 76 mutations|mutated]] players if they have 1-5+ mutations respectively.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Interactions with the player character==&lt;br /&gt;
===Interactions overview===&lt;br /&gt;

|killable           =yes&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===Quests===&lt;br /&gt;
[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]] - The Chief Researcher is one of the potential targets to kill during this bounty hunting [[Fallout 76 public events|public event]]. He will be protected by [[science school dropout]]s and [[Rust raider]]s. He is included as part of Group 5 for [[Fallout 76 challenges|challenges]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Charlie Half-Cocked&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a hostile bounty hunting target, found in the [[Burning Springs (region)|Burning Springs]] region of [[Appalachia]] {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
Charlie Half-Cocked is a hostile individual who appears to prefer using heavy weapons, and wears armor associated with the [[Fanatics]]. He is accompanied by [[vicious sharpshooter]]s. He has earned a bounty placed on his head for some misdeeds. He is wanted dead, and is one of several potential targets of [[the Ghoul]] and the [[Vault Dweller (Fallout 76)|Vault 76 dwellers]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
Charlie wields a [[Gauss rifle (Fallout 76)|Gauss rifle]] with a +300% damage bonus multiplier, as well as the [[Instigating Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Instigating]], [[Executioner&amp;#039;s Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Executioner&amp;#039;s]], and [[Explosive Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Explosive]] [[Fallout 76 legendary weapon effects|legendary weapon effects]]. It also possesses the &amp;quot;Sluggish&amp;quot; effect, setting its fire rate to 90%. Charlie also has the [[Chameleon]] mutation, which behaves similarly to when it is used by a player.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Interactions with the player character==&lt;br /&gt;
===Interactions overview===&lt;br /&gt;
{{Interactions FO76&lt;br /&gt;
|events involved    =[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]]&lt;br /&gt;

|killable           =yes&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===Quests===&lt;br /&gt;
[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]] - Charlie Half-Cocked is one of the potential targets to kill during this bounty hunting [[Fallout 76 public events|public event]]. He will be protected by [[vicious sharpshooter]]s and [[Rust raider]]s. He is included as part of Group 5 for [[Fallout 76 challenges|challenges]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

&lt;br /&gt;
==Appearances==&lt;br /&gt;
Charlie Half-Cocked appears only {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Behind the scenes==&lt;br /&gt;
&amp;quot;Half-cocked&amp;quot; is a term referring to something that is not fully thought out, or not adequately prepared. It is derived from the [[wikipedia:Half-cock|half-cock]] position of a firearm (usually used in reference to a revolver), in which the hammer is only partially cocked. However, Charlie does not use a revolver, instead wielding a Gauss rifle.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

			<title>Dust devil</title>
			<link>https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Dust_devil</link>
			<guid isPermaLink="false">https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Dust_devil</guid>
			<description>&lt;p&gt;LaymansReign: fixing my own grammar&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr /&gt;
&lt;div&gt;{{Infobox creature FO76&lt;br /&gt;
|games       =FO76,FO76BS&lt;br /&gt;
|image       =FO76CC Dust devil.png&lt;br /&gt;
|type        =&lt;br /&gt;
|class       =&lt;br /&gt;
|edid        =Burn_LCharDustDevil (leveled NPC)&amp;lt;br /&amp;gt;Burn_LvlDustDevil (NPC)&amp;lt;br /&amp;gt;Burn_EncDustDevilTemplate (template)&lt;br /&gt;
|formid      ={{ID|0086721e}} (leveled NPC)&amp;lt;br /&amp;gt;{{ID|0086721f}} (NPC)&amp;lt;br /&amp;gt;{{ID|00847621}} (template)&lt;br /&gt;
}}{{FO76CC}}&lt;br /&gt;
&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Dust devils&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; are a type of [[Fallout 76 creatures|creature]] that were [[Fallout 76 cut content|cut]] from the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Fallout 76]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039; [[Fallout 76 updates|update]] &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Burning Springs (update)|Burning Springs]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039;. They were first added to the game&amp;#039;s files with the aforementioned update, but were not implemented and remain as [[Fallout 76 unused content|unused content]]. Although not living beings, dust devils were set up as NPCs with their own skeleton and skin armor, retrofitted for the purposes of a whirlwind environmental hazard.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
Despite not being living creatures, dust devils would have been encountered as active environmental hazards, technically considered NPCs, presumably in the [[Burning Springs (region)|Burning Springs]] region. [[NIF file]]s exist for a standard dust devil variant, as well as an &amp;quot;[[Abraxodyne Chemical|Abraxodyne]]&amp;quot; variant, presumably associated with [[the Rust]]. The dust devils had various animation [[record file]]s set up, in addition to template NPCs, an NPC skeleton, skin armor, and an NPC [[race]]. There are also magic effect and spell record files (&amp;lt;code&amp;gt;Burn_DustDevilPushEffect&amp;lt;/code&amp;gt;, &amp;lt;code&amp;gt;Burn_crDustDevilCloakEffect&amp;lt;/code&amp;gt; and &amp;lt;code&amp;gt;Burn_crDustDevilPushHazard&amp;lt;/code&amp;gt;) that indicate the dust devils would have had an ability to push the player character away, as well as an ability involving cloaking in some capacity.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
None of these assets related to the dust devils were implemented with the release of the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Burning Springs&amp;#039;&amp;#039; update. Although the associated [[editor ID]]s have not been marked with the &amp;lt;code&amp;gt;zzz&amp;lt;/code&amp;gt; prefix or any other equivalent for cut content, dust devils have not been further developed and appear to have been scrapped.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Appearances==&lt;br /&gt;
Dust devils were cut from the &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Fallout 76]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039; [[Fallout 76 updates|update]] &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Burning Springs (update)|Burning Springs]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039;.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

		<item>
			<title>Commonwealth EBS</title>
			<link>https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Commonwealth_EBS</link>
			<guid isPermaLink="false">https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Commonwealth_EBS</guid>
			<description>&lt;p&gt;Jspoelstra: /* Transcript */&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr /&gt;
&lt;div&gt;{{Infobox radio station&lt;br /&gt;
|games            =FO4&lt;br /&gt;
|image            =&lt;br /&gt;
|map marker image =&lt;br /&gt;
|relay tower      =&lt;br /&gt;
|origin           =[[Gunners plaza]]&lt;br /&gt;
|quests           =&lt;br /&gt;
|cell name        =RelayTowerInt01&lt;br /&gt;
|range            =[[The Commonwealth]]&lt;br /&gt;
|edid             =RadioEyebot&lt;br /&gt;
|formid           ={{ID|000e0b5e}} (quest)&lt;br /&gt;
|refid            ={{ID|000e0b61}} (transmitter)&amp;lt;!--the transmitter&amp;#039;s base object is a generic object, so Form ID not listed--&amp;gt;&lt;br /&gt;
}}{{FO4CC}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Commonwealth EBS&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;,&amp;lt;ref&amp;gt;Name used by the &amp;lt;tt&amp;gt;RadioEyebot&amp;lt;/tt&amp;gt; quest&amp;#039;s &amp;lt;tt&amp;gt;RadioTransmitter&amp;lt;/tt&amp;gt; object, assigned via Message &amp;lt;tt&amp;gt;RadioDisplayEyebot&amp;lt;/tt&amp;gt;.&amp;lt;/ref&amp;gt; short for &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Commonwealth Emergency Broadcast System&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;, is an unused [[Fallout 4 radio stations|radio station]] in [[the Commonwealth]] in [[Timeline (2250-2300)#2287|2287]]. It is also known as &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Eyebot Radio&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;.&amp;lt;ref&amp;gt;Name of the quest &amp;lt;tt&amp;gt;RadioEyebot&amp;lt;/tt&amp;gt;.&amp;lt;/ref&amp;gt;&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
This radio signal cannot be heard in regular gameplay. Its radio station frequency is 117.00. It is active from the start of the game, and its broadcast range spans the entire Commonwealth, with its origin being an invisible radio transmitter on the roof of the [[Gunners plaza]] (though it cannot be shut off). Despite this, it does not appear on the [[Pip-Boy 3000 Mark IV|Pip-Boy]]&amp;#039;s radio menu, and there are no other transmitters in the game that broadcast it.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The signal is intended to be an automated alert about the [[Great War]] from the &amp;quot;Commonwealth Emergency Broadcast System,&amp;quot; warning citizens to seek shelter. Based on its [[Editor ID]], it was intended to be played by certain [[Eyebot (Fallout 4)|Eyebots]], but this does not ever occur, as there are no Eyebots set to use radio frequency 117.00. On the other hand, there &amp;#039;&amp;#039;are&amp;#039;&amp;#039; three generic Eyebots placed in the Commonwealth which have the script &amp;lt;tt&amp;gt;[[EyebotRadioScript.psc]]&amp;lt;/tt&amp;gt; attached; this script is intended to make these three Eyebots broadcast a radio station using frequency 103.00, with a developer comment saying that this is for a station called the &amp;quot;Boston Emergency Broadcast.&amp;quot; It is possible that Eyebots using this script were intended to broadcast the Commonwealth EBS signal, but the mismatch in their radio frequencies means this will never happen.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Transcript==&lt;br /&gt;
The radio signal has two messages which are set up to loop between each other. An emergency broadcast alarm sound effect plays at the beginning of each message.&lt;br /&gt;
[[File:Commonwealth EBS1.ogg|center]]&lt;br /&gt;
{{Quotation|This is the Commonwealth Emergency Broadcast System. This is not a test. A State of Emergency has been declared throughout the Commonwealth. Citizens are advised to proceed to the nearest fallout shelter. If a shelter is unavailable, remain indoors until the crisis has passed. Keep listening for additional broadcasts and updates to current information. Above all, remain calm.}}&lt;br /&gt;
[[File:Commonwealth EBS2.ogg|center]]&lt;br /&gt;
{{Quotation|This is the Commonwealth Emergency Broadcast System. This is not a test. A state of emergency has been declared. All citizens with Vault reservations should proceed there immediately. Repeat, this is not a test. Seek shelter in the nearest underground space possible. Beware of concussive blasts and excessive heat. The Emergency Broadcast System will update with more information as it becomes available.}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Plan: Steady handle&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a [[Fallout 76 plans/Weapon mods|mod plan]] {{in|FO76GF}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Locations==&lt;br /&gt;
The plan has a 20% chance to be awarded from completion of the [[Fallout 76 quests#Gone Fission|daily quest]] [[Big Fish in a Small Pond]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Unlocks==&lt;br /&gt;
The plan unlocks crafting of the [[steady handle]] as a mod for the [[Fallout 76 fishing#Fishing rods|fishing rod]], to be applied at a [[tinker&amp;#039;s workbench]].&lt;br /&gt;

		<item>

&lt;br /&gt;
&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Plan: Improved bearing&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a [[Fallout 76 plans/Weapon mods|mod plan]] {{in|FO76GF}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Locations==&lt;br /&gt;
The plan has a 20% chance to be awarded from completion of the [[Fallout 76 quests#Gone Fission|daily quest]] [[Big Fish in a Small Pond]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Unlocks==&lt;br /&gt;
The plan unlocks crafting of the [[improved bearing]] as a mod for the [[Fallout 76 fishing#Fishing rods|fishing rod]], to be applied at a [[tinker&amp;#039;s workbench]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

The &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Gatling gunsmiths&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; are hostile individuals protecting [[Richie Finesse]], found in the [[Burning Springs (region)|Burning Springs]] region of [[Appalachia]] {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
The Gatling gunsmiths are crazed people with gunsmithing knowledge acting as the posse members accompanying Richie Finesse, a fellow heavy weapons aficionado, whom they will protect to the death.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
The Gatling gunsmiths have the [[Quick Hands (Fallout 76)|Quick Hands]] perk, giving them a 25% chance to trigger a refilling of their gun&amp;#039;s entire ammo capacity. They wield [[Gatling gun]]s with no other special effects.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Interactions with the player character==&lt;br /&gt;
===Interactions overview===&lt;br /&gt;

|killable           =yes&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===Quests===&lt;br /&gt;
[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]] - The Gatling gunsmiths are the posse members protecting [[Richie Finesse]], a potential Group 5 bounty hunting target in this [[Fallout 76 public events|public event]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

==Notes==&lt;br /&gt;
In their [[record file]], the Gatling gunsmiths have the keyword intended for a melee bounty hunting enemy, despite possessing Gatling guns.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Appearances==&lt;br /&gt;
The Gatling gunsmiths appear only {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;

&lt;br /&gt;
&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Richie Finesse&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a hostile bounty hunting target, found in the [[Burning Springs (region)|Burning Springs]] region of [[Appalachia]] {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
Richie Finesse is a hostile individual who appears to prefer using heavy weapons, and is accompanied by [[Gatling gunsmith]]s. He has earned a bounty placed on his head for some misdeeds. He is wanted dead, and is one of several potential targets of [[the Ghoul]] and the [[Vault Dweller (Fallout 76)|Vault 76 dwellers]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
Richie wields a &amp;quot;Scrambler&amp;quot; [[Minigun (Fallout 76)|minigun]] with the [[gilded paint]] and the [[Fallout 76 legendary weapon effects|legendary weapon effects]] [[Anti-armor Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Anti-Armor]] and [[Explosive Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Explosive]]. It also has double the ammo capacity and a +150% damage bonus multiplier.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Interactions with the player character==&lt;br /&gt;
===Interactions overview===&lt;br /&gt;
{{Interactions FO76&lt;br /&gt;
|events involved    =[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]]&lt;br /&gt;
|quests involved    =&lt;br /&gt;
|killable           =yes&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===Quests===&lt;br /&gt;
[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]] - Richie Finesse is one of the potential targets to kill during this bounty hunting [[Fallout 76 public events|public event]]. He will be protected by [[Gatling gunsmith]]s and [[Rust raider]]s. He is included as part of Group 5 for [[Fallout 76 challenges|challenges]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

==Notes==&lt;br /&gt;
* Richie has a height of 0.8, compared to the standard 1.0, making him noticeably shorter than most other NPCs in-game.&lt;br /&gt;
* Despite his full name being Richie Finesse, this character&amp;#039;s &amp;quot;short name&amp;quot; parameter in his [[record file]], as seen in dialogue subtitles, is &amp;quot;Sam.&amp;quot;&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Appearances==&lt;br /&gt;
Richie Finesse appears only {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;

&lt;br /&gt;
&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;The Ace&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a hostile bounty hunting target, found in the [[Burning Springs (region)|Burning Springs]] region of [[Appalachia]] {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
The Ace appears to have once been an aviator of some repute, who is accompanied by [[Loyal wingman|loyal wingmen]], though the specifics of his history as a pilot, if they are true at all, are not elaborated upon. The Ace has earned a bounty placed on his head for some misdeeds. He is wanted dead, and is one of several potential targets of [[the Ghoul]] and the [[Vault Dweller (Fallout 76)|Vault 76 dwellers]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
The Ace wields an [[auto grenade launcher]] with a +300% damage bonus multiplier, as well as the [[Two Shot Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Two Shot]] and [[Quad Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Quad]] [[Fallout 76 legendary weapon effects|legendary weapon effects]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Interactions with the player character==&lt;br /&gt;
===Interactions overview===&lt;br /&gt;
{{Interactions FO76&lt;br /&gt;

}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===Quests===&lt;br /&gt;
* [[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]] - The Ace is one of the potential targets to kill during this bounty hunting [[Fallout 76 public events|public event]]. He will be protected by [[Loyal wingman|loyal wingmen]] and [[Rust raider]]s. He is included as part of Group 3 for [[Fallout 76 challenges|challenges]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Road Kill&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a unique piece of [[Fallout 76 armor|armor]] {{In|FO76IN}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
Road Kill is a unique [[Trapper armor (Fallout 76)|trapper armor]] left arm piece. It comes with the [[Overeater&amp;#039;s Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Overeater&amp;#039;s]], [[Glutton Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Glutton]] and [[Thru-hiker&amp;#039;s Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Thru-hiker&amp;#039;s]] [[Fallout 76 legendary crafting|legendary]] modifications. It also provides the unique effect of restoring health and hunger when consuming animal corpses.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Location==&lt;br /&gt;
Possible reward from [[Grahm&amp;#039;s Meat-Cook|Event: Grahm&amp;#039;s Meat-Cook]].&lt;br /&gt;

		<item>
			<title>Armistice Artifice</title>
			<link>https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Armistice_Artifice</link>
			<guid isPermaLink="false">https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Armistice_Artifice</guid>
			<description>&lt;p&gt;S. Nomad: iw&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr /&gt;
&lt;div&gt;{{FOS}}&lt;br /&gt;
&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Armistice Artifice&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a [[Fallout Shelter quests#Limited time quests|limited time]] multi-stage questline {{In|FOS}}. It was introduced in the 2.4.0 update as one of four seasonal questlines for &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Synthetic Identity]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039;, the fifth [[Fallout Shelter seasons|in-game season]]. It consists of four sequential quests. This page serves as an overview for these quests.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
During &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Synthetic Identity&amp;#039;&amp;#039;, Armistice Artifice is associated with the arrival of [[Liam Binet]] to the Experimental Vault. Its prerequisites include having 80 dwellers and completing &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Betrayal of Steel]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039;.&lt;br /&gt;

The &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;brutal bashers&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; are hostile individuals protecting [[Gentle Gary]], found in the [[Burning Springs (region)|Burning Springs]] region of [[Appalachia]] {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
The brutal bashers are the posse members accompanying Gentle Gary, whom they will protect to the death.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
The boxers wield [[Super sledge (Fallout 76)|super sledges]] with the [[Vampire&amp;#039;s Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Vampire&amp;#039;s]] [[Fallout 76 legendary weapon effects|legendary weapon effect]]. The super sledges also have the &amp;quot;Quack&amp;#039;s&amp;quot; effect, which deals increased physical damage against players the higher their [[Hit Points]].&lt;br /&gt;

&lt;br /&gt;
&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Gentle Gary&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a hostile bounty hunting target, found in the [[Burning Springs (region)|Burning Springs]] region of [[Appalachia]] {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
Gary is a rogue, hostile individual who is protected by [[brutal basher]]s. He has earned a bounty placed on his head for some misdeeds and is wanted dead, as one of several potential targets of [[the Ghoul]] and the [[Vault Dweller (Fallout 76)|Vault 76 dwellers]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
Gentle Gary wields a [[Flatliner]] [[Gauss rifle (Fallout 76)|Gauss rifle]] with a +300% damage bonus multiplier, and the [[Fallout 76 legendary weapon effects|legendary weapon effects]] [[Assassin&amp;#039;s Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Assassin&amp;#039;s]], [[V.A.T.S. Enhanced Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|V.A.T.S. Enhanced]], [[Nimble Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Nimble]], and [[Conductor&amp;#039;s Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Conductor&amp;#039;s]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Interactions with the player character==&lt;br /&gt;
===Interactions overview===&lt;br /&gt;
{{Interactions FO76&lt;br /&gt;
|events involved    =[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]]&lt;br /&gt;

|killable           =yes&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===Quests===&lt;br /&gt;
[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]] - Gentle Gary is one of the potential targets to kill during this bounty hunting [[Fallout 76 public events|public event]]. He will be protected by [[brutal basher]]s and [[Rust raider]]s. He is included as part of Group 7 for [[Fallout 76 challenges|challenges]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

{{Quotation|You got it.}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;188 junk vendor&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;,{{Ref|(Note)}} named as a &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;merchant&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; in-game, is a [[Fallout: New Vegas cut content#Character|cut non-player character]] still present in the files of &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Fallout: New Vegas]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039;.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
This person was intended to be a merchant who sold junk at the [[188 Trading Post]]. Unlike other characters cut from the location, he does not have any reference placed in the game world. However, he still has a vendor container ([[Editor ID]] &amp;lt;tt&amp;gt;VendorContainer188Junk&amp;lt;/tt&amp;gt;, [[Form ID]] {{ID|000e666f}}) which has a reference in the [[New Vegas Buried Vendor Chests]] developer cell. The chest includes several leveled items, indicating that the merchant would have sold three [[Fallout: New Vegas weapons#Melee weapons|melee weapons]] and 15 units of [[Fallout: New Vegas miscellaneous items|miscellaneous items]] and/or [[Wasteland outfit (Fallout: New Vegas)|wastelander clothes]] every time his inventory restocked.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

		<item>
			<title>Fish&#039;n hole outfit</title>
			<link>https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Fish%27n_hole_outfit</link>
			<guid isPermaLink="false">https://fallout.fandom.com/wiki/Fish%27n_hole_outfit</guid>
			<description>&lt;p&gt;GamingBro23: added images :)&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr /&gt;
&lt;div&gt;{{Infobox apparel FO76&lt;br /&gt;
|games       =FO76,FO76IN&lt;br /&gt;
|type        =Outfit&lt;br /&gt;
|image       =Score_s25_apparel_outfit_fishnhole_l.webp&lt;br /&gt;

|value       =0&lt;br /&gt;
|tradeable   =no&lt;br /&gt;
|edid        =SCORE_S25_Clothes_ARMO_Outfit_FishnHole&lt;br /&gt;
|formid      ={{ID|008aff62}}&lt;br /&gt;
|keywords    =* {{keyword fo76|ObjectTypeClothing}}&lt;br /&gt;
* {{keyword fo76|UnsellableObject}}&lt;br /&gt;
|name2       =Fish&amp;#039;n hole hat&lt;br /&gt;
|type2        =Headwear&lt;br /&gt;
|image2       =Score_s25_apparel_headwear_fishnholehat_l.webp&lt;br /&gt;
|slots2       =hat&lt;br /&gt;
|weight2      =0.1&lt;br /&gt;
|value2       =0&lt;br /&gt;
|tradeable2   =no&lt;br /&gt;
|edid2        =SCORE_S25_ARMO_Headwear_FishnHoleHat&lt;br /&gt;
|formid2      ={{ID|008d922e}}&lt;br /&gt;
|keywords2    =* {{keyword fo76|ClothingTypeHat }}&lt;br /&gt;
* {{keyword fo76|ClothingTypeHeadwear }}&lt;br /&gt;
* {{keyword fo76|ObjectTypeClothing }}&lt;br /&gt;
* {{keyword fo76|UnsellableObject}}&lt;br /&gt;
}}{{ATX}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
{{Quotation|The best gear for casting and catching!}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
The &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;fish&amp;#039;n hole outfit&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; and &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;fish&amp;#039;n hole hat&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; are an [[Fallout 76 armor and apparel#Outfits|outfit set]] {{In|FO76IN}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
The outfit comprises a white shirt with patchwork applied to it, blue overalls that are frayed at the legs and only have one strap, and fishing net and rope tied around the body. The outfit is also barefoot and has no shoes. The hat is a simple straw hat with a red band.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===Crafting===&lt;br /&gt;
{{Crafting table FO76&lt;br /&gt;
|type        =production&lt;br /&gt;
|material1   =[[Cloth (Fallout 76)|Cloth]]&lt;br /&gt;
|matcnt1     =5&lt;br /&gt;
|workspace   =[[Armor workbench (Fallout 76)|Armor workbench]]&lt;br /&gt;
|learn method=season&lt;br /&gt;
|season      =25&lt;br /&gt;
|product1    =Fish&amp;#039;n hole outfit&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
{{Crafting table FO76&lt;br /&gt;
|type        =production&lt;br /&gt;
|material1   =[[Cloth (Fallout 76)|Cloth]]&lt;br /&gt;
|matcnt1     =3&lt;br /&gt;
|workspace   =[[Armor workbench (Fallout 76)|Armor workbench]]&lt;br /&gt;
|learn method=season&lt;br /&gt;
|season      =25&lt;br /&gt;
|product1    =Fish&amp;#039;n hole hat&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Locations==&lt;br /&gt;
* {{Icon|ticket}} The fish&amp;#039;n hole outfit can only be crafted at an [[Armor workbench (Fallout 76)|armor workbench]] after being purchased as a reward on page 4 of &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[Appalachia Under Siege]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039;, the twenty-fifth [[Fallout 76 seasons|in-game season]], for 60 tickets.&lt;br /&gt;
* {{Icon|ticket}} The fish&amp;#039;n hole hat can only be crafted at an armor workbench after being purchased as a reward on page 4 of &amp;#039;&amp;#039;Appalachia Under Siege&amp;#039;&amp;#039;, the twenty-fifth in-game season, for 30 tickets.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Behind the scenes==&lt;br /&gt;
This outfit closely resembles pop culture depictions of [[wikipedia:Huckleberry Finn|Huckleberry &amp;quot;Huck&amp;quot; Finn]], a major character in the [[wikipedia:Mark Twain|Mark Twain]] novels &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[wikipedia:The Adventures of Tom Sawyer|The Adventures of Tom Sawyer]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039; and &amp;#039;&amp;#039;[[wikipedia:Adventures of Huckleberry Finn|Adventures of Huckleberry Finn]]&amp;#039;&amp;#039;. The similarities include a straw hat, overalls with only one strap, and bare feet.&lt;br /&gt;

The &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;irradiated raiders&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; are hostile individuals protecting [[Irene the Irradiated]], found in the [[Burning Springs (region)|Burning Springs]] region of [[Appalachia]] {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
The irradiated raiders are hostile individuals affiliated with a [[Raiders|raider]] band led by [[Irene the Irradiated]], and have been affected by [[radiation]]. They will protect Irene the Irradiated to the death.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
The irradiated raiders wield [[Pump action shotgun (Fallout 76)|pump action shotguns]] with the [[Mutant&amp;#039;s Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Mutant&amp;#039;s]] and [[Swift Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Swift]] [[Fallout 76 legendary weapon effects|legendary weapon effects]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Interactions with the player character==&lt;br /&gt;
===Interactions overview===&lt;br /&gt;
{{Interactions FO76&lt;br /&gt;
|events involved    =[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]]&lt;br /&gt;
|quests involved    =&lt;br /&gt;
|killable           =yes&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
===Quests===&lt;br /&gt;
[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]] - The irradiated raiders are the posse members protecting [[Irene the Irradiated]], a potential Group 2 bounty hunting target in this [[Fallout 76 public events|public event]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Inventory==&lt;br /&gt;

&lt;br /&gt;
==Notes==&lt;br /&gt;
Although other posse members of Head Hunt targets may have varied, randomized appearances, the irradiated raiders appear to always spawn as ghouls.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Appearances==&lt;br /&gt;
The irradiated raiders appear only {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;

&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;Irene the Irradiated&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a hostile bounty hunting target, found in the [[Burning Springs (region)|Burning Springs]] region of [[Appalachia]] {{in|FO76BS}}.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Background==&lt;br /&gt;
Irene the Irradiated and her posse members, the [[irradiated raider]]s, are people who have experienced the effects of [[radiation]], though Irene herself has not undergone [[ghoul]]ification and remains a [[human]], albeit with significant hair loss. Irene has earned a bounty placed on her head for some misdeeds. She is wanted dead, and is one of several potential targets of [[the Ghoul]] and the [[Vault Dweller (Fallout 76)|Vault 76 dwellers]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
In the encounter against Irene the Irradiated, every player who enters the radius of the fight has the effects of the [[Unstable Isotope]] [[Fallout 76 mutations|mutation]] temporarily applied to them. It gives players a 5% chance to release a radiation blast when struck in melee, dealing fire and radiation damage. Irene also emits a poison effect from her person, similar to [[Plague Walker]]. She wields a [[Hunting rifle (Fallout 76)|hunting rifle]] with a +300% damage bonus multiplier, an extra 40 radiation damage, and the [[Crippling Legendary mod (Fallout 76)|Crippling legendary mod]].&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

===Quests===&lt;br /&gt;
[[Head Hunt|Bounty Hunting: Head Hunt]] - Irene the Irradiated is one of the potential targets to kill during this bounty hunting [[Fallout 76 public events|public event]]. She will be protected by [[irradiated raider]]s and [[Rust raider]]s. She is included as part of Group 2 for [[Fallout 76 challenges|challenges]].&lt;br /&gt;

The &amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039;rusted car table&amp;#039;&amp;#039;&amp;#039; is a [[Fallout 76 workshop objects|CAMP/workshop object]] {{In|FO76BS}}. &lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Characteristics==&lt;br /&gt;
This item comprises the front of an [[off-road vehicle]], taken apart and converted into a functional table. Some road signs have been placed atop and on the sides of the car table. Some noninteractive items, such as a toolbox, have been tucked into a compartment underneath the table, formerly the insides of the car.&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;

|material2   =[[Steel (Fallout 76)|Steel]]&lt;br /&gt;
|matcnt2     =2&lt;br /&gt;

|product1    =Rusted car table&lt;br /&gt;
}}&lt;br /&gt;
&lt;br /&gt;
==Locations==&lt;br /&gt;
* Crafting of this object is unlocked upon completion of the [[Fallout 76 challenges|challenge]] to complete 76 [[Grunt Hunt]]s. However, it is preceded by two other challenges, making the total amount of Grunt Hunts required to unlock crafting 136. Once unlocked, it can be built at any [[CAMP]] or [[Workshop (Fallout 76)|workshop]].&lt;br /&gt;
* A static rusted car table appears at the [[Last Resort]] in [[Highway Town]].&lt;br /&gt;
